-- ==========================================
--  UI SOUND HELPERS & NOTIFICATION ENGINE
-- ==========================================
local function playClickSound()
    pcall(function()
        local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://6895079853"
        sound.Volume = 0.3
        sound.Parent = SoundService
        sound:Play()
        sound.Ended:Connect(function() sound:Destroy() end)
    end)
end

local function notify(title, content, duration)
    pcall(function()
        duration = duration or 3
        local parentGui = (typeof(gethui) == "function") and gethui() or CoreGui
        local notifHolder = parentGui:FindFirstChild("ObsidianGlass_NotifHolder")
        if not notifHolder then
            notifHolder = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            notifHolder.Name = "ObsidianGlass_NotifHolder"
            notifHolder.ResetOnSpawn = false
            notifHolder.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            notifHolder.Parent = parentGui
        end

        local toast = Instance.new("Frame")
        toast.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 60)
        toast.Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 1, -80)
        toast.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.glass
        toast.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        toast.BorderSizePixel = 0
        toast.ZIndex = 999999
        toast.Parent = notifHolder

        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        corner.Parent = toast

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 74, 80)
        stroke.Thickness = 1.5
        stroke.Parent = toast

        local tTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        tTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20)
        tTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 6)
        tTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tTitle.Text = title
        tTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        tTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        tTitle.TextSize = 13
        tTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        tTitle.Parent = toast

        local tDesc = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        tDesc.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 28)
        tDesc.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 26)
        tDesc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tDesc.Text = content
        tDesc.TextColor3 = COLORS.text
        tDesc.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        tDesc.TextSize = 11
        tDesc.TextWrapped = true
        tDesc.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        tDesc.Parent = toast

        playClickSound()
        TweenService:Create(toast, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = UDim2.new(1, -300, 1, -80) }):Play()
        task.delay(duration, function()
            if toast and toast.Parent then
                local tw = TweenService:Create(toast, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), { Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 1, -80) })
                tw:Play()
                tw.Completed:Connect(function() toast:Destroy() end)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

-- Avatar Loader Helper
local customAvatarAsset = nil
local function loadCustomAvatarImage()
    if customAvatarAsset then return customAvatarAsset end
    local avatarUrl = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/aslamdunk7/paypmboygang/main/543199739_2812856088914181_3062917809445648175_n.jpg"
    local fileName = "NAPHUB_avatar.jpg"

    pcall(function()
        if typeof(writefile) == "function" and (typeof(getcustomasset) == "function" or typeof(getsynasset) == "function") then
            local getAsset = getcustomasset or getsynasset
            local isFileExist = (typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(fileName))
            if not isFileExist then
                local imageBytes = game:HttpGet(avatarUrl)
                if imageBytes and #imageBytes > 0 then
                    writefile(fileName, imageBytes)
                end
            end
            if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(fileName) then
                customAvatarAsset = getAsset(fileName)
            end
        end
    end)

    if not customAvatarAsset then
        customAvatarAsset = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. LocalPlayer.UserId .. "&w=150&h=150"
    end
    return customAvatarAsset
end

-- ==========================================
--  LOGOUT & KEY CLEARING & SCRIPT TERMINATION
-- ==========================================
local function performLogoutKeyClear()
    pcall(function()
        local filesToDelete = {
            "NAPHUB_LuarmorKey.txt",
            "NAPHUB_VVIPKey.txt",
            "NAPHUB_SavedKey.txt"
        }
        if LuarmorConfig and type(LuarmorConfig) == "table" and LuarmorConfig.SavedKeyFile then
            table.insert(filesToDelete, LuarmorConfig.SavedKeyFile)
        end

        local del = (type(delfile) == "function" and delfile) or (type(deletefile) == "function" and deletefile)
        for _, file in ipairs(filesToDelete) do
            pcall(function()
                if isfile and isfile(file) then
                    if del then
                        del(file)
                    elseif type(writefile) == "function" then
                        writefile(file, "")
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if getgenv then
            getgenv().script_key = nil
            getgenv().NAPHUB_InputKey = nil
            getgenv().NAPHUB_LoggedOut = true
        end
        if getrenv then pcall(function() getrenv().script_key = nil end) end
        if getfenv then pcall(function() getfenv().script_key = nil end) end
        if _G then _G.script_key = nil end
        if shared then shared.script_key = nil end
        script_key = nil
    end)
end

local WarZ = {} -- ฟังก์ชัน ลดดีด / ปั๊มยาออโต้ (ตัวจริงอยู่ท้ายเมนู)

local function stopAllScriptOperations()
    pcall(function()
        Settings.ESPEnabled = false
        Settings.TeamCheck = true
        Settings.FPSBoost = false
        Settings.HighlightESP = false
        Settings.BoxESP = false
        Settings.SkeletonESP = false
        Settings.NameESP = false
        Settings.HPESP = false
        Settings.ShowLocalPlayer = false
        Settings.AimbotEnabled = false
        Settings.ShowFOV = false
        Settings.Noclip = false
        Settings.NoRecoil = false
        Settings.AutoHeal = false
        Settings.AutoLoot = false
        Settings.AutoFish = false
        Settings.AutoFarmLoot = false
        Settings.InfiniteStamina = false
        if WarZ and WarZ.SetInfiniteStamina then pcall(function() WarZ.SetInfiniteStamina(false) end) end
        Settings.BunnyHop = false
        Settings.FastWeaponSwap = false
        if WarZ and WarZ.SetFastWeaponSwap then pcall(function() WarZ.SetFastWeaponSwap(false) end) end
        Settings.LootESP = false
        if WarZ and WarZ.SetAutoFish then pcall(function() WarZ.SetAutoFish(false) end) end
        if WarZ and WarZ.SetAutoFarmLoot then pcall(function() WarZ.SetAutoFarmLoot(false) end) end
        if WarZ and WarZ.RestoreRecoil then pcall(WarZ.RestoreRecoil) end
        for _, lbl in pairs(lootLabels or {}) do pcall(function() lbl:Destroy() end) end
        lootLabels = {}

        if toggleFPSBoost then
            pcall(function() toggleFPSBoost(false) end)
        end

        for _, line in pairs(tracers) do pcall(function() line:Destroy() end) end
        for _, label in pairs(espLabels) do pcall(function() label:Destroy() end) end
        for _, b in pairs(espBoxes) do pcall(function() b:Destroy() end) end
        tracers = {}
        espLabels = {}
        espBoxes = {}

        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= player and p.Character then
                local hl = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NAPHUBHighlight")
                if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) end
                for _, partName in ipairs({"HumanoidRootPart", "Torso", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"}) do
                    local part = p.Character:FindFirstChild(partName)
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and part:FindFirstChild("OrigSize") then
                        part.Size = part.OrigSize.Value
                        if part.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then part.Transparency = 1 else part.Transparency = 0 end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function() if _G.NapHubExtraCleanup then _G.NapHubExtraCleanup() end end)
end

if _G.NapHubCleanup then
    pcall(_G.NapHubCleanup)
    task.wait(0.1)
end

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
while not LocalPlayer do
    task.wait(0.05)
    LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
end

-- GUI container resolver
local cloneref = cloneref or function(i) return i end
local CoreGui = cloneref(game:GetService("CoreGui"))
local function getSafeGuiContainer()
    if gethui then
        local ok, h = pcall(gethui)
        if ok and typeof(h) == "Instance" then return h end
    end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local t = Instance.new("Folder"); t.Parent = CoreGui; t:Destroy()
    end)
    if ok then return CoreGui end
    return LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
end

local function safeDestroyGui(name)
    pcall(function()
        local parents = { CoreGui, LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") }
        if gethui then pcall(function() table.insert(parents, gethui()) end) end
        for _, p in ipairs(parents) do
            if p then
                for _, c in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
                    if c.Name == name then pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
safeDestroyGui("NapHub_LootToForge_Floating")
safeDestroyGui("NapHubUI")
safeDestroyGui("WarZ_LootESP")

_G.NapHubConns = {}
local RUN = {}            -- token ของการรันรอบนี้ (ใช้หยุด loop ของรอบเก่า)
_G.NapHubRun = RUN

local function bind(signal, fn)
    local c = signal:Connect(fn)
    table.insert(_G.NapHubConns, c)
    return c
end

_G.NapHubCleanup = function()
    _G.NapHubRun = nil
    pcall(stopAllScriptOperations)
    for _, c in ipairs(_G.NapHubConns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    _G.NapHubConns = {}
    pcall(function() game:GetService("RunService"):UnbindFromRenderStep("NapHubAimbot") end)
    pcall(function() game:GetService("RunService"):UnbindFromRenderStep("NapHubDrawESP") end)
    if _G.NapHubDrawClear then pcall(_G.NapHubDrawClear) end
    safeDestroyGui("NapHub_LootToForge_Floating")
    safeDestroyGui("NapHubUI")
    safeDestroyGui("ESP_Tracers")
    safeDestroyGui("NAPHUBFOV")
    safeDestroyGui("WarZ_LootESP")
end

local function b64decode(s)
    local chars = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
    s = s:gsub("%s", "")
    local lut = {}
    for i = 1, #chars do lut[chars:byte(i)] = i - 1 end
    local out, k = {}, 0
    for i = 1, #s, 4 do
        local a, b, c, d = s:byte(i, i + 3)
        local n = lut[a] * 262144 + lut[b] * 4096 + (lut[c] or 0) * 64 + (lut[d] or 0)
        local b1 = math.floor(n / 65536)
        local b2 = math.floor(n / 256) % 256
        local b3 = n % 256
        k = k + 1
        if c == 61 then
            out[k] = string.char(b1)
        elseif d == 61 then
            out[k] = string.char(b1, b2)
        else
            out[k] = string.char(b1, b2, b3)
        end
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

-- เขียนรูปที่ฝังไว้ลงไฟล์ แล้วคืนค่า asset ที่ใช้กับ Image ได้ (ถ้าทำไม่ได้คืน "")
local function loadAsset(fileName, b64)
    if not (writefile and getcustomasset) then return "" end
    local result = ""
    pcall(function()
        writefile(fileName, b64decode(b64))
        result = getcustomasset(fileName)
    end)
    return result
end

-- ====================================================================
-- UI HELPERS (Metal Dragon Theme)
-- ====================================================================
local function uiNew(class, props, parent)
    local o = Instance.new(class)
    for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
    o.Parent = parent
    return o
end

local function uiCorner(parent, r)
    return uiNew("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }, parent)
end

local function uiStroke(parent, th, color)
    return uiNew("UIStroke", {
        Thickness = th,
        Color = color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
    }, parent)
end

-- ไล่เฉดสีโลหะเงิน
local function uiMetal(parent, rot)
    return uiNew("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = rot or 45,
        Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(240, 241, 245)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, Color3.fromRGB(130, 132, 138)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.65, Color3.fromRGB(200, 202, 208)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(95, 97, 103)),
        }),
    }, parent)
end

-- เพชรสี่เหลี่ยมขนมเปียกปูน (เหมือนในโลโก้)
local function uiDiamond(parent, size, pos, z)
    local d = uiNew("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = pos,
        Rotation = 45,
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = z or 5,
    }, parent)
    uiMetal(d, 45)
    uiStroke(d, 1, Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 14))
    return d
end

-- ทำให้ลาก/แตะได้ (แตะ = onTap, ลาก = ย้ายตำแหน่ง ไม่เด้งเปิดเมนู)
local function uiDraggable(handle, target, onTap)
    local dragging, moved, dragStart, startPos = false, false, nil, nil
    handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging, moved = true, false
            dragStart, startPos = input.Position, target.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                    if not moved and onTap then onTap() end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    bind(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
            local d = input.Position - dragStart
            if d.Magnitude > 6 then moved = true end
            if moved then
                target.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end
        end
    end)
end

local ActiveTab = nil
local Tabs = {}
local TabButtons = {}

local function makeLabel(parent, text, size, color)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, size or 24)
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = tostring(text or "")
    l.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
    l.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    l.TextSize = 12
    l.TextWrapped = true
    l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.Parent = parent
    return l
end

local function addStroke(obj, color)
    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    st.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 76)
    st.Thickness = 1
    st.Transparency = 0.15
    st.Parent = obj
    return st
end

local function controlButton(parent, text, height)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height or 38)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 33)
    b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.Text = tostring(text or "")
    b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 234, 237)
    b.TextSize = 11
    b.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    b.TextWrapped = true
    b.Parent = parent
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
    c.Parent = b
    addStroke(b)
    return b
end

local function makeTab(title, icon)
    local tab = {
        Title = title,
        Icon = icon,
        Controls = {},
        Frame = Instance.new("Frame"),
    }
    tab.Frame.Name = "Tab_" .. tostring(#Tabs + 1)
    tab.Frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    tab.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tab.Frame.Visible = false
    tab.Frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    tab.Frame.Parent = Content

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Parent = tab.Frame

    local tabHeader = Instance.new("Frame")
    tabHeader.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
    tabHeader.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tabHeader.LayoutOrder = -10
    tabHeader.Parent = tab.Frame

    local tabTitle = makeLabel(tabHeader, tostring(title), 26, Color3.fromRGB(243,244,246))
    tabTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0)
    tabTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 22)
    tabTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    tabTitle.TextSize = 13

    local tabHint = makeLabel(tabHeader, "จัดการฟังก์ชันในหมวดนี้", 18, Color3.fromRGB(170,173,180))
    tabHint.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 23)
    tabHint.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 16)
    tabHint.TextSize = 9

    local side = Instance.new("TextButton")
    side.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 31)
    side.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 33)
    side.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    side.BorderSizePixel = 0
    side.AutoButtonColor = false
    side.Text = ""
    side.Parent = Sidebar
    local sc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    sc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
    sc.Parent = side
    local sideStroke = addStroke(side)

    local sideIcon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    sideIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 21, 0, 21)
    sideIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -10)
    sideIcon.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(52, 52, 56)
    sideIcon.Text = string.sub(tostring(title), 1, 1)
    sideIcon.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(215, 218, 222)
    sideIcon.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    sideIcon.TextSize = 10
    sideIcon.Parent = side
    local sic = Instance.new("UICorner")
    sic.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    sic.Parent = sideIcon

    local sideText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    sideText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -31, 1, 0)
    sideText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 29, 0, 0)
    sideText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    sideText.Text = tostring(title)
    sideText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(198, 201, 205)
    sideText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    sideText.TextSize = 9
    sideText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    sideText.Parent = side

    local function renderSide(selected)
        if selected then
            side.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(58, 59, 65)
            sideStroke.Color = Accent
            sideIcon.BackgroundColor3 = Accent
            sideIcon.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 18)
            sideText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        else
            side.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 33)
            sideStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 76)
            sideIcon.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(52, 52, 56)
            sideIcon.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(215, 218, 222)
            sideText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(198, 201, 205)
        end
    end
    renderSide(false)
    tab.RenderSide = renderSide
    table.insert(AccentHooks, function()
        renderSide(ActiveTab == tab)
    end)

    local function selectTab()
        for _, t in ipairs(Tabs) do
            t.Frame.Visible = false
            t.RenderSide(false)
        end
        tab.Frame.Visible = true
        renderSide(true)
        ActiveTab = tab
    end
    side.MouseButton1Click:Connect(selectTab)
    table.insert(Tabs, tab)
    table.insert(TabButtons, side)

    function tab:Select() selectTab() end

    function tab:Section(opt)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12)
        f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.Parent = tab.Frame
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        c.Parent = f
        local l = makeLabel(f, "  " .. tostring(opt and opt.Title or ""), 30, Color3.fromRGB(205,207,213))
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        l.TextSize = 10
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        return f
    end

    function tab:Button(opt)
        local b = controlButton(tab.Frame, opt and opt.Title or "", 40)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if opt and opt.Callback then pcall(opt.Callback) end
        end)
        return b
    end

    -- All boolean settings use this single Toggle Switch UI.
    function tab:Toggle(opt)
        local state = opt and opt.Value == true
        local holder = Instance.new("TextButton")
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        holder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 33)
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
        holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
        holder.AutoButtonColor = false
        holder.Text = ""
        holder.Parent = tab.Frame
        local hc = Instance.new("UICorner")
        hc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        hc.Parent = holder
        addStroke(holder)

        local label = makeLabel(holder, opt and opt.Title or "", 40, Color3.fromRGB(229,231,234))
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 3)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, -6)
        label.TextSize = 10
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center

        local stateText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        stateText.Size = UDim2.new(0, 27, 0, 18)
        stateText.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, -8)
        stateText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        stateText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        stateText.TextSize = 7
        stateText.Parent = holder

        local track = Instance.new("Frame")
        track.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20)
        track.Position = UDim2.new(1, -45, 0.5, -10)
        track.BorderSizePixel = 0
        track.Parent = holder
        local tc = Instance.new("UICorner")
        tc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        tc.Parent = track

        local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
        knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        knob.Parent = track
        local kc = Instance.new("UICorner")
        kc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        kc.Parent = knob

        local control = {}
        local function render()
            if state then
                track.BackgroundColor3 = Accent
                knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 246, 248)
                knob.Position = UDim2.new(1, -17, 0.5, -7)
                stateText.Text = "ON"
                stateText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232,234,237)
            else
                track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(57, 60, 65)
                knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(137, 141, 147)
                knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -7)
                stateText.Text = "OFF"
                stateText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150,155,162)
            end
        end
        table.insert(AccentHooks, render)
        local function set(v, fire)
            state = v == true
            render()
            if fire and opt and opt.Callback then
                pcall(opt.Callback, state)
                autoSaveConfig()
            end
        end
        function control:SetValue(v) set(v, true) end
        function control:Set(v) set(v, true) end
        function control:GetValue() return state end
        holder.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() set(not state, true) end)
        render()
        return control
    end

    function tab:Dropdown(opt)
        local values = opt and opt.Values or {}
        local current = opt and opt.Value
        if current == nil then current = values[1] end
        local multi = opt and opt.Multi == true
        if multi and type(current) ~= "table" then current = current and {current} or {} end
        local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44)
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        holder.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        holder.Parent = tab.Frame
        local b = controlButton(holder, "", 44)
        local title = makeLabel(b, opt and opt.Title or "", 18, Color3.fromRGB(220,222,225))
        title.Position = UDim2.new(0,9,0,2)
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1,-30,0,16)
        title.TextSize = 9
        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        local valueLabel = makeLabel(b, "", 20, Color3.fromRGB(180,184,190))
        valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0,9,0,20)
        valueLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,-30,0,17)
        valueLabel.TextSize = 9
        local arrow = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0,18,0,18)
        arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1,-22,0,13)
        arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        arrow.Text = "›"
        arrow.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190,194,200)
        arrow.TextSize = 15
        arrow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        arrow.Parent = b
        local listFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        listFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
        listFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14,14,16)
        listFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
        listFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        listFrame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        listFrame.Visible = false
        listFrame.Parent = holder
        local lc = Instance.new("UICorner")
        lc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
        lc.Parent = listFrame
        local lp = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        lp.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0,4); lp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0,4)
        lp.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,4); lp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,4)
        lp.Parent = listFrame
        local ll = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        ll.Padding = UDim.new(0,2)
        ll.Parent = listFrame
        local function currentText()
            if multi then
                local a = {}
                for _,v in ipairs(current) do table.insert(a,tostring(v)) end
                return #a > 0 and table.concat(a, ", ") or "ไม่ได้เลือก"
            end
            return tostring(current or "")
        end
        valueLabel.Text = currentText()
        local function fire()
            if opt and opt.Callback then pcall(opt.Callback,current); autoSaveConfig() end
        end
        local control = {}
        function control:Select(v) current=v; if multi and type(v)~="table" then current={v} end; valueLabel.Text=currentText(); fire() end
        function control:Set(v) self:Select(v) end
        function control:SetValue(v) self:Select(v) end
        function control:GetValue() return current end
        for _,v in ipairs(values) do
            local vb = controlButton(listFrame,tostring(v),27)
            vb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if multi then
                    local found=false
                    for i,x in ipairs(current) do if x==v then table.remove(current,i); found=true; break end end
                    if not found then table.insert(current,v) end
                else current=v; listFrame.Visible=false end
                valueLabel.Text=currentText(); fire()
            end)
        end
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            listFrame.Visible=not listFrame.Visible
            arrow.Text=listFrame.Visible and "⌄" or "›"
        end)
        return control
    end

    function tab:Slider(opt)
        local min,max,default=0,100,0
        if opt and type(opt.Value)=="table" then min=tonumber(opt.Value.Min) or min; max=tonumber(opt.Value.Max) or max; default=tonumber(opt.Value.Default) or min else default=tonumber(opt and opt.Value) or min end
        local step=tonumber(opt and opt.Step) or 1
        if step<=0 then step=1 end
        local value=math.clamp(default,min,max)

        local b=controlButton(tab.Frame,"",52)
        local title=makeLabel(b,opt and opt.Title or "",16,Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220))
        title.Position=UDim2.new(0,9,0,4); title.Size=UDim2.new(1,-80,0,16); title.TextSize=9; title.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
        local valLabel=makeLabel(b,tostring(value),16,Color3.fromRGB(200,203,208))
        valLabel.Position=UDim2.new(1,-69,0,4); valLabel.Size=UDim2.new(0,60,0,16); valLabel.TextSize=9; valLabel.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right

        local track=Instance.new("Frame")
        track.Size=UDim2.new(1,-24,0,6); track.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0,34)
        track.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(57,60,65); track.BorderSizePixel=0; track.Parent=b
        local tcr=Instance.new("UICorner"); tcr.CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0); tcr.Parent=track
        local fill=Instance.new("Frame")
        fill.Size=UDim2.new(0,0,1,0); fill.BackgroundColor3=Accent; fill.BorderSizePixel=0; fill.Parent=track
        local fcr=Instance.new("UICorner"); fcr.CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0); fcr.Parent=fill
        table.insert(AccentHooks, function() fill.BackgroundColor3 = Accent end)
        local knob=Instance.new("Frame")
        knob.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5); knob.Size=UDim2.new(0,14,0,14)
        knob.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(245,246,248); knob.BorderSizePixel=0; knob.ZIndex=2; knob.Parent=track
        local kcr=Instance.new("UICorner"); kcr.CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0); kcr.Parent=knob

        local function render()
            local a=(max>min) and ((value-min)/(max-min)) or 0
            fill.Size=UDim2.new(a,0,1,0)
            knob.Position=UDim2.new(a,0,0.5,0)
            valLabel.Text=tostring(value)
        end
        local function set(v,fire)
            local raw=math.clamp(tonumber(v) or value,min,max)
            local snapped=min+math.floor(((raw-min)/step)+0.5)*step
            snapped=math.floor(math.clamp(snapped,min,max)*1000+0.5)/1000
            local changed=(snapped~=value)
            value=snapped
            render()
            if fire and changed and opt and opt.Callback then pcall(opt.Callback,value); autoSaveConfig() end
        end

        local dragging=false
        local function fromX(x)
            local w=math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X,1)
            local a=math.clamp((x-track.AbsolutePosition.X)/w,0,1)
            set(min+a*(max-min),true)
        end
        local function setScroll(on) pcall(function() Content.ScrollingEnabled=on end) end
        b.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging=true; setScroll(false); fromX(i.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        bind(UserInputService.InputChanged,function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then fromX(i.Position.X) end
        end)
        bind(UserInputService.InputEnded,function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then dragging=false; setScroll(true) end
        end)

        render()
        local control={}
        function control:SetValue(v) set(v,true) end
        function control:Set(v) set(v,true) end
        function control:GetValue() return value end
        return control
    end

    function tab:Paragraph(opt)
        local f=Instance.new("Frame")
        f.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,52); f.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(30,30,33); f.BackgroundTransparency=0.1; f.BorderSizePixel=0; f.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y; f.Parent=tab.Frame
        local c=Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,9); c.Parent=f
        addStroke(f)
        local t=makeLabel(f,opt and opt.Title or "",22,Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235)); t.Position=UDim2.new(0,9,0,3); t.Size=UDim2.new(1,-18,0,19); t.TextSize=10; t.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
        local body=makeLabel(f,opt and opt.Content or "",30,Color3.fromRGB(190,192,198)); body.Position=UDim2.new(0,9,0,22); body.Size=UDim2.new(1,-18,0,27); body.TextSize=9
        local control={}; function control:SetContent(v) body.Text=tostring(v or "") end
        return control
    end
    return tab
end

local function setOpen(open)
    if open then
        MainScale.Scale = 0.92
        MainFrame.Visible = true
        TweenService:Create(MainScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
    else
        MainFrame.Visible = false
    end
end

-- Window compatibility layer: keeps the original Tab(...) calls working.
local ToggleGui
local Window = {}
function Window:Tab(opt)
    return makeTab(opt and opt.Title or "เมนู", opt and opt.Icon or "")
end
function Window:Open()
    setOpen(true)
end
function Window:Close()
    setOpen(false)
end
function Window:Destroy()
    pcall(function() ScreenGui:Destroy() end)
    pcall(function() ToggleGui:Destroy() end)
end
function Window:SelectTab(tab)
    if tab and tab.Select then tab:Select() end
end

local lastToggleTime = 0
local function toggleMainWindow()
    local now = tick()
    if now - lastToggleTime < 0.22 then return end
    lastToggleTime = now
    if MainFrame.Visible then
        Window:Close()
    else
        Window:Open()
        task.defer(function()
            if Tabs[1] then Tabs[1]:Select() end
        end)
    end
end

CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Window:Close() end)

-- ====================================================================
-- FLOATING BUTTON (ลูกลอย) ใช้รูปโลโก้ NAP จริง
-- ====================================================================
ToggleGui = uiNew("ScreenGui", {
    Name = "NapHub_LootToForge_Floating",
    ResetOnSpawn = false,
    DisplayOrder = 2147483647,
    ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
    IgnoreGuiInset = true,
}, GuiParent)

local BTN_SIZE = 48 -- ขนาดลูกลอย (พิกเซล) ปรับเลขนี้ได้
local FloatingBtn = uiNew("TextButton", {
    Name = "FloatingToggleButton",
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BTN_SIZE, BTN_SIZE),
    Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -BTN_SIZE / 2),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Text = "",
    AutoButtonColor = false,
    Active = true,
}, ToggleGui)

do
    -- แบบวาดเอง (สำรอง ใช้เมื่อโหลดรูปไม่ได้)
    local Fallback = uiNew("Frame", {
        Name = "Fallback",
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 34, 37),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, FloatingBtn)
    uiCorner(Fallback, 14)
    uiNew("UIScale", { Scale = BTN_SIZE / 64 }, Fallback)
    uiNew("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 62), Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 24)),
    }, Fallback)
    uiMetal(uiStroke(Fallback, 3, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), 45)

    local Inner = uiNew("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, Fallback)
    uiCorner(Inner, 10)
    uiStroke(Inner, 1, Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 92))

    local Ring1 = uiNew("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, Fallback)
    uiCorner(Ring1, 23)
    uiMetal(uiStroke(Ring1, 2.5, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), 135)

    local NapText = uiNew("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        Text = "NAP",
        TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        TextSize = 19,
        Font = Enum.Font.Fantasy,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        ZIndex = 3,
    }, Fallback)
    uiMetal(NapText, 90)

    uiDiamond(Fallback, 8, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -23), 4)
    uiDiamond(Fallback, 8, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 23), 4)
    uiDiamond(Fallback, 8, UDim2.new(0.5, -23, 0.5, 0), 4)
    uiDiamond(Fallback, 8, UDim2.new(0.5, 23, 0.5, 0), 4)

    -- รูปโลโก้จริง
    local logoImage = LOGO_IMAGE
    if logoImage == "" then
        logoImage = loadAsset("naphub_logo_v3.png", LOGO_B64)
    end

    if logoImage ~= "" then
        local Img = uiNew("ImageLabel", {
            Name = "Logo",
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image = logoImage,
            ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
            ZIndex = 10,
        }, FloatingBtn)
        task.spawn(function()
            local t = 0
            while not Img.IsLoaded and t < 5 do
                task.wait(0.1)
                t = t + 0.1
            end
            if Img.IsLoaded then
                Fallback.Visible = false
            else
                Img:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- แตะ = เปิด/ปิดเมนู | ลาก = ย้ายลูกลอย
uiDraggable(FloatingBtn, FloatingBtn, toggleMainWindow)

-- Keep the original state-driven visibility option.
if State.FloatingButtonVisible == false then
    ToggleGui.Enabled = false
end

-- เปลี่ยนสีธีม (UI ล้วน)
local function applyThemePreset(name)
    local c = ThemePresets[name]
    if not c then return end
    Accent = c
    for _, hook in ipairs(AccentHooks) do
        pcall(hook)
    end
end
applyThemePreset(State.CurrentTheme)

local function noop() end

-- ====================================================================
-- ORIGINAL LOGOUT FUNCTION (logic preserved)
-- ====================================================================
local gui = ScreenGui
-- Logout :  /  key /  Start
local function performOriginalLogout()
    playClickSound()
    stopAllScriptOperations()
    performLogoutKeyClear()

    pcall(function()
        local pg = (typeof(gethui) == "function") and gethui() or CoreGui
        local tracerG = pg:FindFirstChild("ESP_Tracers")
        local fovG = pg:FindFirstChild("NAPHUBFOV")
        local notifH = pg:FindFirstChild("ObsidianGlass_NotifHolder")
        local lootG = pg:FindFirstChild("WarZ_LootESP")
        if tracerG then tracerG:Destroy() end
        if fovG then fovG:Destroy() end
        if notifH then notifH:Destroy() end
        if lootG then lootG:Destroy() end
    end)

    if gui and gui.Parent then
        gui:Destroy()
    end
    if ToggleGui and ToggleGui.Parent then
        ToggleGui:Destroy()
    end

    pcall(function()
        loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/aslamdunk7/paypmboygang/refs/heads/main/Start"))()
    end)
end

    Icon = "home"

})

local function createObsidianToggle(name, iconText, titleText, descText, defaultValue, callback)
    local control = MainTab:Toggle({
        Title = tostring(iconText or "") .. "  " .. tostring(titleText or "") ..
            "  •  " .. tostring(descText or ""),
        Value = defaultValue == true,
        Callback = function(state)
            if callback then
                callback(state)
            end
        end
    })
    return control
end

    end

SettingsTab:Button({
    Title = "Rejoin",
    Callback = function()
        local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
        if #Players:GetPlayers() <= 1 then
            TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
        else
            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
        end
    end
})

SettingsTab:Button({
    Title = "Server Hop",
    Callback = function()
        local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
        local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local success, result = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/0?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"))
        end)
        if success and result and result.data then
            for _, server in ipairs(result.data) do
                if server.id ~= game.JobId and server.playing < server.maxPlayers then
                    TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, server.id, LocalPlayer)
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
    end
})
