-- ====================================================================
--  WARZ: ENGINE ENHANCEMENT SUITE (ANTI-CHEAT BYPASS & AUTOMATION)
-- ====================================================================
do
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 1. NO RECOIL & NO SPREAD (SAFE MEMORY RESTORATION)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    local RECOIL_FIELDS = {
        "Recoil", "RecoilUp", "RecoilLeft", "RecoilRight",
        "CameraKick", "CameraKickUp", "CameraKickHorizontal",
        "VisualRecoil", "AimRecoilMultiplier",
        "Spread", "MinSpread", "MaxSpread", "BaseSpread",
    }
    local saved = {}

    local function applyNoRecoil()
        if not getgc then return end
        pcall(function()
            for _, v in pairs(getgc(true)) do
                if type(v) == "table" then
                    for _, key in ipairs(RECOIL_FIELDS) do
                        local cur = rawget(v, key)
                        if cur ~= nil and cur ~= 0 then
                            if #saved < 5000 then
                                saved[#saved + 1] = { v, key, cur }
                            end
                            pcall(rawset, v, key, 0)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function WarZ.RestoreRecoil()
        for _, e in ipairs(saved) do
            pcall(rawset, e[1], e[2], e[3])
        end
        saved = {}
    end

    function WarZ.SetNoRecoil(state)
        Settings.NoRecoil = state
        if state then
            task.spawn(applyNoRecoil)
        else
            WarZ.RestoreRecoil()
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NapHubRun == RUN do
            if Settings.NoRecoil then applyNoRecoil() end
            task.wait(5)
        end
    end)

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 2. SMART AUTO HEAL (RATE-LIMITED & THRESHOLD-SAFE)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    local healRemoteCache = nil
    local warnedNoHealRemote = false
    local lastHealTime = 0

    local function getUseItemRemote()
        if healRemoteCache and healRemoteCache.Parent then return healRemoteCache end
        healRemoteCache = nil
        local remotes = RS:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local r = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("UseItem")
        if not r then
            for _, d in ipairs(RS:GetDescendants()) do
                if d.Name == "UseItem" and (d:IsA("RemoteEvent") or d:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then
                    r = d
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        healRemoteCache = r
        return r
    end

    local function getMyHumanoid()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    end

    local function needHeal(hum)
        return hum and hum.Parent and hum.Health > 0 and hum.MaxHealth > 0
            and (hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth) <= Settings.AutoHealThreshold
    end

    local function tryHeal()
        if not Settings.AutoHeal then return end
        local hum = getMyHumanoid()
        if not needHeal(hum) then return end

        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastHealTime < Settings.AutoHealCooldown then return end
        lastHealTime = now

        local useItem = getUseItemRemote()
        if not useItem then
            if not warnedNoHealRemote then
                warnedNoHealRemote = true
                notify("Auto Heal", "ไม่พบ Remote UseItem ในเกมนี้", 4)
            end
            return
        end
        warnedNoHealRemote = false

        pcall(function()
            if useItem:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                useItem:InvokeServer(3, Settings.AutoHealItem, nil, nil, true)
            else
                useItem:FireServer(3, Settings.AutoHealItem, nil, nil, true)
            end
        end)
    end

    function WarZ.SetAutoHeal(state)
        Settings.AutoHeal = state
        if state then
            lastHealTime = 0
            tryHeal()
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NapHubRun == RUN do
            if Settings.AutoHeal then tryHeal() end
            task.wait(math.max(Settings.AutoHealCooldown, 0.2))
        end
    end)

    local function watchCharHeal(char)
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if not hum then return end
        bind(hum.HealthChanged, function()
            tryHeal()
        end)
    end

    bind(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(char)
        task.spawn(watchCharHeal, char)
    end)
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        task.spawn(watchCharHeal, LocalPlayer.Character)
    end

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 3. SMART AUTO LOOT V2 (DISTANCE BYPASS & NON-BLOCKING)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    local PICKUP_ARG3 = 3
    local triedLoot = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local lootBusy = false
    local warnedNoId = false

    local function isUuid(v)
        return type(v) == "string"
            and v:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x$") ~= nil
    end

    local function getLootId(inst)
        -- เช็ค Attribute LootUid ซึ่งเป็นตำแหน่งจริงที่เซิร์ฟเวอร์เก็บ ID
        local uid = inst:GetAttribute("LootUid")
        if uid and isUuid(uid) then return uid end
        if isUuid(inst.Name) then return inst.Name end
        for _, v in pairs(inst:GetAttributes()) do
            if isUuid(v) then return v end
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
            if isUuid(d.Name) then return d.Name end
            if d:IsA("StringValue") and isUuid(d.Value) then return d.Value end
            for _, v in pairs(d:GetAttributes()) do
                if isUuid(v) then return v end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function getLootFolder()
        return workspace:FindFirstChild("WarzLoot")
    end

    local function getLootPosition(inst)
        if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst.Position end
        if inst:IsA("Model") then
            local pp = inst.PrimaryPart or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            if pp then return pp.Position end
            local ok, cf = pcall(function() return inst:GetPivot() end)
            if ok and cf then return cf.Position end
        end
        local part = inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        return part and part.Position or nil
    end

    local function getMyRoot()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local function lootDist(inst)
        local root = getMyRoot()
        local pos = inst.Parent and getLootPosition(inst)
        if not root or not pos then return math.huge end
        return (pos - root.Position).Magnitude
    end

    local function nearestLoot()
        local folder = getLootFolder()
        if not folder then return nil end
        local best, bestD = nil, Settings.AutoLootRange
        local now = os.clock()
        for _, inst in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            if not (triedLoot[inst] and now - triedLoot[inst] < 1.8) then
                local d = lootDist(inst)
                if d <= bestD then
                    best, bestD = inst, d
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    local function pickup(loot)
        local remotes = RS:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local remote = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("PickupLoot")
        if not remote then return end

        local id = getLootId(loot)
        if not id then
            if not warnedNoId then
                warnedNoId = true
                local attrs = {}
                for k, v in pairs(loot:GetAttributes()) do
                    attrs[#attrs + 1] = tostring(k) .. "=" .. tostring(v)
                end
                notify("Auto Loot", "หา ID ของไม่เจอ: " .. loot.Name, 5)
            end
            return
        end

        pcall(function() remote:FireServer(id, "prep", PICKUP_ARG3) end)

        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < Settings.AutoLootHold do
            if _G.NapHubRun ~= RUN or not Settings.AutoLoot then return end
            if not loot.Parent or lootDist(loot) > Settings.AutoLootRange + 3 then return end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end

        pcall(function() remote:FireServer(id, "use", PICKUP_ARG3) end)
    end

    function WarZ.SetAutoLoot(state)
        Settings.AutoLoot = state
        if state then warnedNoId = false end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NapHubRun == RUN do
            if Settings.AutoLoot and not lootBusy then
                local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local loot = nearestLoot()
                    if loot then
                        lootBusy = true
                        triedLoot[loot] = os.clock()
                        pcall(pickup, loot)
                        lootBusy = false
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(math.max(Settings.AutoLootDelay, 0.1))
        end
    end)

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 4. WARZ AUTO FISHING SYSTEM (PERFECT GREEN REEL & AUTO REPEAT)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    local WarZFishing = {
        CurrentPhase = "idle",
        CastId = nil,
        StatusText = "พร้อมใช้งาน (ปิดอยู่)",
    }
    local fishingBusy = false

    local function updateFishingStatus(txt)
        WarZFishing.StatusText = txt
        if FishingStatusParagraph and FishingStatusParagraph.SetContent then
            pcall(function() FishingStatusParagraph:SetContent(txt) end)
        end
    end

    local function findEquippedOrBagRod(char)
        local activeSlot = tonumber(LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("CSGO_ActiveSlot")) or 0
        local catalogId = tostring(LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("CSGO_CatalogId") or "")
        if catalogId:lower():find("rod") then
            return activeSlot
        end
        return activeSlot > 0 and activeSlot or 1
    end

    local function triggerNextCast()
        if not Settings.AutoFish or _G.NapHubRun ~= RUN then return end
        if fishingBusy then return end
        fishingBusy = true

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
            updateFishingStatus("ตัวละครเสียชีวิต หยุดการตกปลา")
            fishingBusy = false
            return
        end

        -- วิธีที่ 1: เรียกผ่าน world.Fishing แท้ของเกม (ให้อนิเมชั่นและระบบเสียงทำงานปกติ)
        local fishMod = nil
        pcall(function()
            local ps = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
            local cl = ps and ps:FindFirstChild("Client")
            local w = cl and cl:FindFirstChild("world")
            if w and w:FindFirstChild("Fishing") then
                fishMod = require(w.Fishing)
            end
        end)

        if fishMod and type(fishMod.Press) == "function" then
            updateFishingStatus("กำลังเหวี่ยงเบ็ด (Game Native)...")
            pcall(function() fishMod.Press() end)
            task.wait(0.5)
            fishingBusy = false
            return
        end

        -- วิธีที่ 2: Fallback ยิง Remote ตรง
        local rem = RS:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local fStart = rem and rem:FindFirstChild("FishingStart")
        if fStart then
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local forward = (cam and cam.CFrame.LookVector) or (root and root.CFrame.LookVector) or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
            local castPos = (root and root.Position or Vector3.zero) + Vector3.new(forward.X, 0, forward.Z).Unit * 25
            local rodSlot = findEquippedOrBagRod(char)
            local baitSlot = 5

            updateFishingStatus("กำลังเหวี่ยงเบ็ด (Remote Fallback)...")
            pcall(function()
                fStart:FireServer(rodSlot, baitSlot, castPos)
            end)
        else
            updateFishingStatus("ไม่พบ Remote FishingStart ในเกม")
        end

        task.wait(0.5)
        fishingBusy = false
    end

    -- Hook Remote รับสถานะ FishingState จากเซิร์ฟเวอร์
    local function setupFishingRemoteHook()
        local rem = RS:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local fState = rem and rem:FindFirstChild("FishingState")
        local fReel = rem and rem:FindFirstChild("FishingReel")
        if not fState or not fReel then return end

        bind(fState.OnClientEvent, function(data)
            if not Settings.AutoFish or _G.NapHubRun ~= RUN then return end
            if type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.phase) ~= "string" then return end

            if data.phase == "cast" then
                WarZFishing.CurrentPhase = "cast"
                WarZFishing.CastId = data.castId
                updateFishingStatus("เหวี่ยงเบ็ดแล้ว... กำลังรอปลากินเบ็ด (Waiting for bite)")

            elseif data.phase == "reel" then
                WarZFishing.CurrentPhase = "reel"
                WarZFishing.CastId = data.castId
                updateFishingStatus("ปลากินเบ็ดแล้ว! กำลังตวัดเข้าแถบเขียว (Reeling)...")

                task.spawn(function()
                    local castId = data.castId
                    local biteAt = data.biteAt or Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                    local speed = data.speed or 1
                    local zoneCenter = data.zoneCenter or 0.5
                    local zoneWidth = data.zoneWidth or 0.3
                    local deadline = data.deadline or (Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + 4)

                    if Settings.FishPerfectReel then
                        -- คำนวณความถี่การสวิงของ Marker ให้ลงกลาง ZoneCenter พอดีเป๊ะ (Perfect Catch 100%)
                        local tStart = os.clock()
                        while Settings.AutoFish and (os.clock() - tStart < 3.2) and (Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() < deadline) do
                            local tNow = Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                            local cycle = (tNow - biteAt) * speed % 2
                            if cycle < 0 then cycle = cycle + 2 end
                            local marker = (cycle < 1) and cycle or (2 - cycle)
                            local diff = math.abs(marker - zoneCenter)
                            if diff <= (zoneWidth * 0.38) then
                                pcall(function()
                                    fReel:FireServer(castId, tNow)
                                end)
                                updateFishingStatus("ตวัดปลาสำเร็จ! (Perfect Reel 100%)")
                                return
                            end
                            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        end
                    end

                    pcall(function()
                        fReel:FireServer(castId, Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
                    end)
                end)

            elseif data.phase == "result" then
                WarZFishing.CurrentPhase = "result"
                if data.ok then
                    local rewardStr = "ไอเทมปลา"
                    if type(data.rewards) == "table" and #data.rewards > 0 then
                        local r1 = data.rewards[1]
                        rewardStr = tostring(r1.name or r1.itemId or "ปลา") .. " x" .. tostring(r1.qty or 1)
                    elseif data.name or data.itemId then
                        rewardStr = tostring(data.name or data.itemId) .. " x" .. tostring(data.qty or 1)
                    end
                    updateFishingStatus("ตกปลาสำเร็จ: " .. rewardStr .. " (รอเริ่มรอบใหม่)")
                    notify("Auto Fish", "ตกปลาสำเร็จ! ได้รับ: " .. rewardStr, 2.5)
                else
                    updateFishingStatus("ปลาหลุด: " .. tostring(data.message or "หลุดมือ") .. " (รอรอบใหม่)")
                end

                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(math.max(Settings.FishCastDelay, 0.85))
                    if Settings.AutoFish and _G.NapHubRun == RUN then
                        triggerNextCast()
                    end
                end)

            elseif data.phase == "cancel" then
                WarZFishing.CurrentPhase = "idle"
                updateFishingStatus("การตกปลาถูกยกเลิก (รอ 1.5 วินาทีเพื่อเริ่มใหม่)")
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(1.5)
                    if Settings.AutoFish and _G.NapHubRun == RUN then
                        triggerNextCast()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    function WarZ.SetAutoFish(state)
        Settings.AutoFish = state
        if state then
            updateFishingStatus("เปิดใช้งาน Auto Fish แล้ว กำลังเริ่มตกปลา...")
            setupFishingRemoteHook()
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.3)
                triggerNextCast()
            end)
        else
            updateFishingStatus("ปิดระบบ Auto Fish แล้ว")
            pcall(function()
                local rem = RS:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                local fCancel = rem and rem:FindFirstChild("FishingCancel")
                if fCancel and WarZFishing.CastId then
                    fCancel:FireServer(WarZFishing.CastId)
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 5. LOOT ESP (มองเห็นกล่องของและอาวุธดรอปบนพื้น)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    lootEspGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    lootEspGui.Name = "WarZ_LootESP"
    lootEspGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    lootEspGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    lootEspGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    lootEspGui.Parent = GuiParent

    local function getLootLabel(inst)
        if lootLabels[inst] then return lootLabels[inst] end
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Name = "LootItemLabel"
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.TextSize = 12
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
        lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
        lbl.ZIndex = 10
        lbl.Visible = false
        lbl.Parent = lootEspGui
        lootLabels[inst] = lbl
        return lbl
    end

    local function cleanLootLabels()
        for inst, lbl in pairs(lootLabels) do
            if not inst or not inst.Parent then
                if lbl then pcall(function() lbl:Destroy() end) end
                lootLabels[inst] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    function WarZ.SetLootESP(state)
        Settings.LootESP = state
        if not state then
            for _, lbl in pairs(lootLabels) do lbl.Visible = false end
        end
    end

    local lastLootClean = 0
    bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
        if not Settings.LootESP then
            for _, lbl in pairs(lootLabels) do lbl.Visible = false end
            return
        end

        local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local char = player.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not Camera or not root then return end

        local lootFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("WarzLoot")
        if not lootFolder then return end

        local nowClean = os.clock()
        if nowClean - lastLootClean > 1 then
            lastLootClean = nowClean
            cleanLootLabels()
        end

        local rootPos = root.Position
        for _, item in ipairs(lootFolder:GetChildren()) do
            local pos = nil
            if item:IsA("BasePart") then
                pos = item.Position
            elseif item:IsA("Model") then
                local pp = item.PrimaryPart or item:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                pos = pp and pp.Position
            end

            if pos then
                local dist = (pos - rootPos).Magnitude
                if dist <= Settings.LootMaxDistance then
                    local screenPos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
                    if onScreen and screenPos.Z > 0 then
                        local lbl = getLootLabel(item)
                        local name = item:GetAttribute("LootName") or item:GetAttribute("LootItemId") or item.Name
                        lbl.Text = string.format("[📦] %s\n(%dm)", tostring(name), math.floor(dist))
                        lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(screenPos.X - 60, screenPos.Y - 10)
                        lbl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 26)
                        lbl.Visible = true
                    else
                        if lootLabels[item] then lootLabels[item].Visible = false end
                    end
                else
                    if lootLabels[item] then lootLabels[item].Visible = false end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 6. STORAGE & AMMO HELPERS (STASH & COMBINE MAGS)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    function WarZ.DepositAllToStash()
        local rem = RS:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local r = rem and rem:FindFirstChild("BagAllToStash")
        if r then
            pcall(function() r:FireServer() end)
            notify("คลังเซฟโซน", "ส่งคำสั่งฝากของทั้งหมดลงคลังแล้ว", 3)
        else
            notify("คลังเซฟโซน", "ไม่พบ Remote BagAllToStash", 3)
        end
    end

    function WarZ.CombineMagazines()
        local rem = RS:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local r = rem and rem:FindFirstChild("CombineMags")
        if r then
            pcall(function() r:FireServer() end)
            notify("กระสุน", "ส่งคำสั่งรวมซองกระสุนแล้ว", 3)
        else
            notify("กระสุน", "ไม่พบ Remote CombineMags", 3)
        end
    end

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 7. AUTO FARM LOOT RUNNER (AUTONOMOUS LOOT RUNNER)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    local farmRunnerBusy = false

    local function updateFarmStatus(txt)
        if FarmLootStatusParagraph and FarmLootStatusParagraph.SetContent then
            pcall(function() FarmLootStatusParagraph:SetContent(txt) end)
        end
    end

    local function isEnemyNearby(range)
        local char = player.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return false, nil end
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= player and not isSameTeam(p) then
                local pChar = getCharacter(p)
                local pRoot = pChar and pChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local pHum = pChar and pChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if pRoot and pHum and pHum.Health > 0 then
                    if (pRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude <= range then
                        return true, p
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return false, nil
    end

    local function isItemMatchFilter(inst)
        local name = tostring(inst:GetAttribute("LootName") or inst:GetAttribute("LootItemId") or inst.Name):lower()
        local filter = Settings.FarmLootFilter or "ของมีค่าทั้งหมด (ปืน เกราะ ยา แม็ก)"

        if filter:find("เก็บทุกอย่าง") then
            return true
        elseif filter:find("ปืนและชุดเกราะ") then
            return name:find("armor") or name:find("helmet") or name:find("shield") or name:find("m4")
                or name:find("stanag") or name:find("ak") or name:find("awp") or name:find("svd")
                or name:find("honey") or name:find("blaser") or name:find("mossberg") or name:find("vss")
        else
            return name:find("armor") or name:find("helmet") or name:find("shield") or name:find("medkit")
                or name:find("bandage") or name:find("stanag") or name:find("cmag") or name:find("mag")
                or name:find("honey") or name:find("m4") or name:find("ak") or name:find("awp")
                or name:find("svd") or name:find("rod") or name:find("bait") or name:find("backpack")
                or name:find("stamina") or name:find("blaser")
        end
    end

    local function findBestFarmLoot()
        local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("WarzLoot")
        if not folder then return nil, math.huge end
        local root = getMyRoot()
        if not root then return nil, math.huge end

        local best, bestDist = nil, Settings.FarmLootDistance
        local rootPos = root.Position

        for _, inst in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            if not (triedLoot[inst] and (os.clock() - triedLoot[inst] < 3)) then
                if isItemMatchFilter(inst) then
                    local p = getLootPosition(inst)
                    if p then
                        local d = (p - rootPos).Magnitude
                        if d <= bestDist then
                            bestDist = d
                            best = inst
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return best, bestDist
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NapHubRun == RUN do
            if Settings.AutoFarmLoot and not farmRunnerBusy then
                local char = player.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if hum and root and hum.Health > 0 then
                    if Settings.FarmStopOnEnemy then
                        local danger, enemy = isEnemyNearby(70)
                        if danger and enemy then
                            updateFarmStatus("⚠️ ระวัง! มีศัตรูใกล้ตัว (" .. enemy.Name .. ") หยุดฟาร์มชั่วคราว")
                            task.wait(1)
                            continue
                        end
                    end

                    local target, dist = findBestFarmLoot()
                    if target and target.Parent then
                        local itemName = target:GetAttribute("LootName") or target:GetAttribute("LootItemId") or target.Name
                        updateFarmStatus(string.format("กำลังเดินไปหา: %s (ระยะ %d m)", tostring(itemName), math.floor(dist)))

                        farmRunnerBusy = true
                        local startTime = os.clock()

                        while Settings.AutoFarmLoot and target.Parent and (os.clock() - startTime < 8) do
                            if Settings.FarmStopOnEnemy and isEnemyNearby(60) then
                                updateFarmStatus("⚠️ ตรวจพบศัตรู! ยกเลิกการเดินไปหาของ")
                                break
                            end

                            local targetPos = getLootPosition(target)
                            if not targetPos then break end
                            local curDist = (targetPos - root.Position).Magnitude
                            if curDist <= 9.0 then
                                updateFarmStatus("ถึงจุดแล้ว! กำลังดูดเก็บ: " .. tostring(itemName))
                                triedLoot[target] = os.clock()
                                pcall(pickup, target)
                                task.wait(0.2)
                                break
                            end

                            local dir = (targetPos - root.Position).Unit
                            local stepSpeed = Settings.FarmLootSpeed or 26
                            hum:MoveTo(targetPos)

                            if curDist > 9.0 then
                                root.CFrame = root.CFrame + (dir * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)) * (stepSpeed * 0.03)
                            end

                            task.wait(0.03)
                        end
                        farmRunnerBusy = false
                    else
                        updateFarmStatus("ไม่พบของที่เข้าเงื่อนไขในระยะ " .. tostring(Settings.FarmLootDistance) .. " studs")
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end)

    function WarZ.SetAutoFarmLoot(state)
        Settings.AutoFarmLoot = state
        if state then
            Settings.AutoLoot = true
            updateFarmStatus("เปิดระบบ Auto Farm Runner แล้ว กำลังสแกนของ...")
        else
            farmRunnerBusy = false
            updateFarmStatus("ปิดระบบ Auto Farm แล้ว")
        end
    end

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 8. INFINITE STAMINA, BUNNY HOP & FAST WEAPON SWAP
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------
    local STAMINA_ATTRS = {
        CSGO_Stamina = 100,
        CSGO_SprintPenalty = 0,
        WarzServerSprintAllowed = true,
    }

    local function pinStamina()
        for k, v in pairs(STAMINA_ATTRS) do
            if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute(k) ~= v then
                LocalPlayer:SetAttribute(k, v)
            end
        end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        for _, holder in ipairs({ LocalPlayer, char }) do
            if holder then
                for name, val in pairs(holder:GetAttributes()) do
                    if type(val) == "number" and tostring(name):lower():find("stamina") then
                        local maxv = tonumber(holder:GetAttribute(name .. "Max"))
                            or tonumber(holder:GetAttribute("MaxStamina")) or 100
                        if val < maxv then holder:SetAttribute(name, maxv) end
                    end
                end
                for _, v in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("IntValue") then
                        if v.Name:lower():find("stamina") and v.Value < 100 then
                            v.Value = 100
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function WarZ.SetInfiniteStamina(state)
        Settings.InfiniteStamina = state
        if state then
            pcall(pinStamina)
            StaminaPatcher.run()
        else
            StaminaPatcher.restore()
        end
    end

    bind(LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("CSGO_Stamina"), function()
        if Settings.InfiniteStamina and LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("CSGO_Stamina") ~= 100 then
            pcall(pinStamina)
        end
    end)

    local lastPin = 0
    bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        if not Settings.InfiniteStamina then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastPin < 0.1 then return end
        lastPin = now
        pcall(pinStamina)
    end)

    -- Bunny Hop
    local lastHop = 0
    local NO_HOP_STATES = {
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll] = true,
    }

    function WarZ.SetBunnyHop(state)
        Settings.BunnyHop = state
    end

    bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        if not Settings.BunnyHop then return end
        if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        if not UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then return end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or hum.Sit then return end
        if NO_HOP_STATES[hum:GetState()] then return end
        if hum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastHop < 0.05 then return end
        lastHop = now
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
        hum.Jump = true
    end)

    -- Config patcher (ใช้ร่วมกันระหว่าง Fast Swap / Infinite Stamina)
    local function lowerHas(str, list)
        for _, w in ipairs(list) do
            if str:find(w, 1, true) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local SWAP_WORDS = { "swap", "switch", "equip", "draw", "deploy", "holster" }
    local TIME_WORDS = { "time", "delay", "cooldown", "duration", "lock" }
    local DRAIN_WORDS = { "drain", "cost", "consum", "usage", "persecond", "persec" }

    local function matchSwapKey(k)
        local l = k:lower()
        return lowerHas(l, SWAP_WORDS) and lowerHas(l, TIME_WORDS)
    end

    local function matchStaminaKey(k)
        local l = k:lower()
        if not (l:find("stamina", 1, true) or l:find("sprint", 1, true)) then return false end
        if l:find("regen", 1, true) or l:find("max", 1, true) then return false end
        return lowerHas(l, DRAIN_WORDS)
    end

    -- โมดูลของเกมที่น่าจะเก็บค่า config (ได้จาก dump)
    local function gameConfigModules()
        local list = {}
        local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local shared = RS:FindFirstChild("Shared")
        if shared then
            for _, n in ipairs({ "MovementTiming", "Config", "Inventory", "WarzData" }) do
                local m = shared:FindFirstChild(n)
                if m and m:IsA("ModuleScript") then table.insert(list, m) end
            end
        end
        local ps = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
        local client = ps and ps:FindFirstChild("Client")
        if client then
            for _, n in ipairs({ "PlayerStance", "CombatSettings", "CombatInput", "WorldHold", "WarzAnimator", "FpsView", "PlayerAnim" }) do
                local m = client:FindFirstChild(n, true)
                if m and m:IsA("ModuleScript") then table.insert(list, m) end
            end
        end
        return list
    end

    local function newPatcher(label, matchFn, newValue)
        local P = { store = {}, token = 0, count = 0 }

        local function patchTable(tbl, path, visited, depth)
            if visited[tbl] or depth > 4 then return end
            visited[tbl] = true
            for k, v in pairs(tbl) do
                if type(k) == "string" and type(v) == "number" and v ~= newValue and matchFn(k) then
                    P.store[tbl] = P.store[tbl] or {}
                    if P.store[tbl][k] == nil then P.store[tbl][k] = v end
                    if pcall(rawset, tbl, k, newValue) then
                        P.count += 1
                        print(("[NAP HUB][%s] %s.%s : %s -> %s"):format(label, path, k, tostring(v), tostring(newValue)))
                    end
                elseif type(v) == "table" then
                    patchTable(v, path .. "." .. tostring(k), visited, depth + 1)
                end
            end
        end

        function P.run()
            P.token += 1
            local token = P.token
            P.count = 0
            task.spawn(function()
                -- 1) โมดูลของเกม (ตรงจุดที่สุด)
                for _, m in ipairs(gameConfigModules()) do
                    local ok, data = pcall(require, m)
                    if ok and type(data) == "table" then
                        pcall(patchTable, data, m.Name, {}, 0)
                    end
                end
                -- 2) getgc สำรอง (ตารางที่หลุดจากโมดูล)
                if type(getgc) == "function" then
                    local ok, gc = pcall(getgc, true)
                    if ok and type(gc) == "table" then
                        local visited = {}
                        for i, obj in ipairs(gc) do
                            if token ~= P.token then return end
                            if type(obj) == "table" and not P.store[obj] then
                                pcall(patchTable, obj, "gc", visited, 4)
                            end
                            if i % 2000 == 0 then task.wait() end
                        end
                    end
                end
                pcall(function()
                    notify(label, ("ปรับค่าในเกมแล้ว %d จุด"):format(P.count), 3)
                end)
            end)
        end

        function P.restore()
            P.token += 1
            for tbl, fields in pairs(P.store) do
                for k, v in pairs(fields) do pcall(rawset, tbl, k, v) end
            end
            P.store = {}
        end

        return P
    end

    local SwapPatcher = newPatcher("Fast Swap", matchSwapKey, 0)
    local StaminaPatcher = newPatcher("Stamina", matchStaminaKey, 0)

    -- Fast Weapon Swap
    local SWAP_ANIM_WORDS = { "equip", "draw", "swap", "switch", "holster", "deploy", "unequip" }
    local SWAP_ANIM_SPEED = 6

    local function speedUpSwapTrack(track)
        if not Settings.FastWeaponSwap then return end
        local nm = (tostring(track.Name) .. " " .. tostring(track.Animation and track.Animation.Name or "")):lower()
        for _, w in ipairs(SWAP_ANIM_WORDS) do
            if nm:find(w, 1, true) then
                pcall(function() track:AdjustSpeed(SWAP_ANIM_SPEED) end)
                return
            end
        end
    end

    local function hookSwapAnimator(char)
        task.spawn(function()
            local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            local animator = hum and (hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 10))
            if animator then bind(animator.AnimationPlayed, speedUpSwapTrack) end
        end)
    end

    function WarZ.SetFastWeaponSwap(state)
        Settings.FastWeaponSwap = state
        if state then SwapPatcher.run() else SwapPatcher.restore() end
    end

    if LocalPlayer.Character then hookSwapAnimator(LocalPlayer.Character) end
    bind(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, hookSwapAnimator)
end

local RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)

    st.Thickness = 2
    st.Parent = b

    hpBg.Name = "HPBG"
    hpBg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    hpBg.Position = UDim2.new(0, -3, 0, 0)
    hpBg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, 0)
    hpBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
    hpBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hpBg.ZIndex = 4
    hpBg.Parent = b
    local hpStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    hpStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    hpStroke.Thickness = 1
    hpStroke.Parent = hpBg
    local hpFill = Instance.new("Frame")
    hpFill.Name = "HPFill"
    hpFill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)

local function createTracer(targetPlayer)
    if targetPlayer == player then return end
    if tracers[targetPlayer] then return end

    local line = Instance.new("Frame")
    line.Name = targetPlayer.Name .. "_Tracer"
    line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    line.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.primary
    line.BorderSizePixel = 0
    line.Visible = false
    line.ZIndex = 5
    line.Parent = tracerGui
    tracers[targetPlayer] = line

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = targetPlayer.Name .. "_Label"
    label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 32)
    label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    label.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.glassDeep
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    label.Text = targetPlayer.Name .. "\n0 studs"
    label.TextColor3 = COLORS.text
    label.TextSize = 10.5
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    label.Visible = false
    label.ZIndex = 6
    label.Parent = tracerGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", label).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    local lStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    lStroke.Color = COLORS.cyan
    lStroke.Thickness = 1
    lStroke.Parent = label
    espLabels[targetPlayer] = label
end

local function removeTracer(targetPlayer)
    if tracers[targetPlayer] then
        tracers[targetPlayer]:Destroy()
        tracers[targetPlayer] = nil
    end
    if espLabels[targetPlayer] then
        espLabels[targetPlayer]:Destroy()
        espLabels[targetPlayer] = nil
    end
    if espBoxes[targetPlayer] then
        espBoxes[targetPlayer]:Destroy()
        espBoxes[targetPlayer] = nil
    end
end

local function getCharacter(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer then return nil end
    if targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
        return targetPlayer.Character
    end
    local charByName = workspace:FindFirstChild(targetPlayer.Name)
    if charByName and charByName:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
        return charByName
    end
    for _, folderName in ipairs({"Characters", "Players", "Entities"}) do
        local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(folderName)
        if folder then
            local c = folder:FindFirstChild(targetPlayer.Name)
            if c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                return c
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function bindPlayerEvents(targetPlayer)
    if targetPlayer == player then return end
    createTracer(targetPlayer)
    targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.1)
        createTracer(targetPlayer)
    end)
end

for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    bindPlayerEvents(p)
end
bind(Players.PlayerAdded, bindPlayerEvents)
bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, removeTracer)

-- FPS Boost Function Logic Implementation
function toggleFPSBoost(state)
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local Terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

    if state then
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
        if Terrain then
            Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
            Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
            Terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
            Terrain.WaterTransparency = 0
        end
        for _, v in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if v:IsA("BasePart") and not v:IsA("Terrain") then
                    v.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                    v.Reflectance = 0
                elseif v:IsA("Decal") or v:IsA("Texture") then
                    v.Transparency = 1
                elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") then
                    v.Enabled = false
                end
            end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            if getgenv and getgenv().setfpscap then
                getgenv().setfpscap(999)
            elseif setfpscap then
                setfpscap(999)
            end
        end)
    else
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
        Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
        pcall(function()
            if getgenv and getgenv().setfpscap then
                getgenv().setfpscap(60)
            elseif setfpscap then
                setfpscap(60)
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- Team Check Logic
local function isSameTeamRaw(targetPlayer)
    if not Settings.TeamCheck then return false end
    if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == player then return true end

    if player.Team ~= nil and targetPlayer.Team ~= nil then
        if player.Team == targetPlayer.Team then
            local teamName = tostring(player.Team.Name):lower()
            if not string.find(teamName, "neutral") and not string.find(teamName, "ffa") and not string.find(teamName, "free") then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    if player.TeamColor and targetPlayer.TeamColor and player.TeamColor == targetPlayer.TeamColor then
        local colorName = tostring(player.TeamColor.Name):lower()
        if not string.find(colorName, "white") and not string.find(colorName, "grey") and not string.find(colorName, "gray") then
            return true
        end
    end

    local myTeamVal = player:FindFirstChild("Team") or (player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats") and player.leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Team"))
    local targetTeamVal = targetPlayer:FindFirstChild("Team") or (targetPlayer:FindFirstChild("leaderstats") and targetPlayer.leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Team"))
    if myTeamVal and targetTeamVal and myTeamVal.Value == targetTeamVal.Value and tostring(myTeamVal.Value) ~= "" and tostring(myTeamVal.Value) ~= "0" then
        return true
    end

    local myChar = player.Character
    local targetChar = targetPlayer.Character
    if myChar and targetChar then
        local myCharTeam = myChar:FindFirstChild("Team") or myChar:FindFirstChild("TeamColor")
        local targetCharTeam = targetChar:FindFirstChild("Team") or targetChar:FindFirstChild("TeamColor")
        if myCharTeam and targetCharTeam and myCharTeam.Value == targetCharTeam.Value then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

local function isSameTeam(targetPlayer)
    local ok, res = pcall(isSameTeamRaw, targetPlayer)
    if ok then return res end
    return false
end

-- เช็ก "มี HP จริง" : เลือด > 0, ไม่อยู่ในสถานะตาย/ล้ม, หลอด HP ไม่ว่างเปล่า
-- ใช้ร่วมกันทั้ง Aimbot (ใกล้/ไกล), Box ESP, Tracer และ Highlight
local MIN_VALID_HP = 1 -- เลือดต่ำกว่านี้ถือว่า "ไม่มี HP" (กันค่าเศษ เช่น 0.01)
local function hasHP(hum)
    if not hum or not hum.Parent then return false end
    local hp, maxHp = hum.Health, hum.MaxHealth
    if hp ~= hp or maxHp ~= maxHp then return false end -- NaN
    if maxHp <= 0 or hp < MIN_VALID_HP then return false end
    if hp / maxHp <= 0.005 then return false end        -- หลอด HP แทบว่าง
    local okS, st = pcall(function() return hum:GetState() end)
    if okS and (st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) then return false end
    return true
end

-- Distance Color Matrix
local function getColorByDistance(dist)
    if dist < 100 then
        return COLORS.danger
    elseif dist < 300 then
        return COLORS.warning
    elseif dist < 600 then
        return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 100)
    else
        return COLORS.success
    end
end

-- Background Task Loop: Highlight
task.spawn(function()
    while _G.NapHubRun == RUN and task.wait(0.25) do
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= player then
                pcall(function()
                    local char = getCharacter(p)
                    if not char then return end

                    local hl = char:FindFirstChild("NAPHUBHighlight")
                    if Settings.HighlightESP then
                        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if humanoid and hasHP(humanoid) and not isSameTeam(p) then
                            if not hl then
                                hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
                                hl.Name = "NAPHUBHighlight"
                                hl.Parent = char
                            end
                            -- Chams: อัปเดตค่าจากแท็บ Chams ทุกรอบ (สไลเดอร์/ปุ่มมีผลทันที)
                            local oT = Settings.ChamsOutline and 0 or 1
                            local dm = Settings.ChamsThroughWalls and Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop or Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
                            if hl.FillColor ~= Settings.ChamsColor then hl.FillColor = Settings.ChamsColor end
                            if hl.OutlineColor ~= Settings.ChamsOutlineColor then hl.OutlineColor = Settings.ChamsOutlineColor end
                            if hl.FillTransparency ~= Settings.ChamsFillTransparency then hl.FillTransparency = Settings.ChamsFillTransparency end
                            if hl.OutlineTransparency ~= oT then hl.OutlineTransparency = oT end
                            if hl.DepthMode ~= dm then hl.DepthMode = dm end
                        else
                            if hl then hl:Destroy() end
                        end
                    else
                        if hl then hl:Destroy() end
                    end

                end)
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function makeInfoBox(tab, text)
    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    box.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(31, 34, 38)
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.Parent = tab.Frame
    local bc = Instance.new("UICorner"); bc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9); bc.Parent = box
    addStroke(box)
    local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6); pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8); pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pad.Parent = box
    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    lbl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    lbl.TextSize = 9
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(175, 175, 180)
    lbl.TextWrapped = true
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    lbl.Text = text or ""
    lbl.Parent = box
    return lbl
end
