local wsHum, wsOrig = nil, nil
local flyBV, flyBG = nil, nil

local function restoreWalkSpeed()
    if wsHum and wsHum.Parent and wsOrig then
        pcall(function() wsHum.WalkSpeed = wsOrig end)
    end
    wsHum, wsOrig = nil, nil
end

local function stopFly()
    if flyBV then pcall(function() flyBV:Destroy() end) flyBV = nil end
    if flyBG then pcall(function() flyBG:Destroy() end) flyBG = nil end
end

bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
    pcall(function()
        local char = player.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        -- WalkSpeed (เซ็ตซ้ำทุกเฟรม เผื่อเกมรีเซ็ตค่ากลับ)
        if Settings.WalkSpeedEnabled and hum then
            if wsHum ~= hum then
                restoreWalkSpeed()
                wsHum = hum
                wsOrig = hum.WalkSpeed
            end
            hum.WalkSpeed = Settings.WalkSpeedValue
        elseif wsHum then
            restoreWalkSpeed()
        end

        -- Fly
        if not (Settings.Fly and hum and root and hum.Health > 0) then
            if flyBV or flyBG then stopFly() end
            return
        end
        if not flyBV or flyBV.Parent ~= root then
            stopFly()
            flyBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            flyBV.Name = "NAPHUBFlyBV"
            flyBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
            flyBV.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            flyBV.Parent = root
            flyBG = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            flyBG.Name = "NAPHUBFlyBG"
            flyBG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
            flyBG.P = 9e4
            flyBG.Parent = root
        end

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        local dir = hum.MoveDirection
        local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
        local flatLook = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        local fwd = 0
        if flatLook.Magnitude > 0.01 then fwd = dir:Dot(flatLook.Unit) end

        local v = Vector3.new(dir.X, look.Y * fwd, dir.Z)
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or hum.Jump then
            v = v + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) then
            v = v - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        end
        if v.Magnitude > 1 then v = v.Unit end

        flyBV.Velocity = v * Settings.FlySpeed
        flyBG.CFrame = cam.CFrame
    end)
end)

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ cleanup hook
_G.NapHubExtraCleanup = function()
    Settings.WalkSpeedEnabled = false
    Settings.Fly = false
    pcall(restoreWalkSpeed)
    pcall(stopFly)
end

-- Stepped Loop: Noclip (แคชรายการ part ไว้ ไม่สแกนทุกเฟรม)
local noclipChar, noclipParts, noclipNext = nil, {}, 0
bind(RunService.Stepped, function()
    pcall(function()
        local char = player.Character
        if char and Settings.Noclip then
            local now = os.clock()
            if noclipChar ~= char or now >= noclipNext then
                noclipChar, noclipNext = char, now + 1
                table.clear(noclipParts)
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then noclipParts[#noclipParts + 1] = part end
                end
            end
            for i = 1, #noclipParts do
                local part = noclipParts[i]
                if part.CanCollide then part.CanCollide = false end
            end
        end
    end)
end)

-- Target Locking & Closest Player Finder
-- ล็อคเป้าเฉพาะเป้าที่อยู่ด้านหน้า + มองเห็นตัวละครจริงเท่านั้น
-- ไม่ล็อคผ่านกำแพง/สิ่งกีดขวาง และไม่ล็อคเป้าหมายที่อยู่นอกระยะ
-- ล็อคเฉพาะตอน "กดยิง/ใช้" เท่านั้น นอกนั้นไม่ล็อค
local snapLock -- กำหนดค่าทีหลัง: หันไปล็อคทันทีเมื่อกดเล็ง/ยิง
-- กฎ: ล็อคเป้า "เฉพาะ" ตอนกดยิง/ใช้อาวุธเท่านั้น ปุ่มอื่นทั้งหมด (รวมเล็ง/ซูม) ไม่มีผล
local mouseShoot, mouseAim = false, false       -- เมาส์ (นับเฉพาะคลิกที่ไม่ได้โดน UI)
local padShoot, padAim = false, false           -- จอย R2 / L2
local activeTouches = {}                        -- [input] = "aim" | "shoot" เฉพาะนิ้วที่กดปุ่มเล็ง/ยิงจริง
local activeTool = nil                          -- อาวุธ Tool ที่กำลังถูกใช้ (Activated)
local GuiServiceX = game:GetService("GuiService")
local touchOnlyDevice = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled and not UserInputService.MouseEnabled

-- มือถือ: นิ้วต้องแตะ "ปุ่ม" (GuiButton) ที่ชื่อเป็นปุ่มเล็ง/ยิงโดยตรง (เช็กแค่ปุ่มนั้นกับพ่อของมัน)
local AIM_WORDS = {"aim", "scope", "zoom", "sight"}
local SHOOT_WORDS = {"fire", "shoot", "attack", "trigger"}
local function nameHas(n, words)
    n = string.lower(n)
    for _, w in ipairs(words) do
        if string.find(n, w, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local function classifyName(n)
    if nameHas(n, AIM_WORDS) or string.lower(n) == "ads" or string.find(string.lower(n), "^ads[_%s]") then return "aim" end
    if nameHas(n, SHOOT_WORDS) then return "shoot" end
    return nil
end
-- ปุ่ม "ยิง / ใช้" ของเกม (WarzMobileControls.TouchHud.Fire / FireLeft)
-- ไม่ใช้การเดาตำแหน่งนิ้วแล้ว (เสี่ยงคลาดเคลื่อนจนกระโดดไปโดนปุ่มยิง)
-- แต่ผูกเหตุการณ์กับตัวปุ่มยิงโดยตรง: ต้องกดโดนปุ่มยิงจริงเท่านั้นถึงจะล็อค
local FIRE_BUTTON_NAMES = { "Fire", "FireLeft" }
local hookedFireBtns = {}
local function hookFireButton(btn)
    if hookedFireBtns[btn] then return end
    hookedFireBtns[btn] = true
    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        local t = input.UserInputType
        if t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            activeTouches[input] = "shoot"
            if snapLock then pcall(snapLock) end
        end
    end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    while _G.NapHubRun == RUN do
        pcall(function()
            local pg = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            local mc = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("WarzMobileControls")
            local hud = mc and mc:FindFirstChild("TouchHud")
            if hud then
                for _, n in ipairs(FIRE_BUTTON_NAMES) do
                    local b = hud:FindFirstChild(n)
                    if b and b:IsA("GuiButton") then hookFireButton(b) end
                end
            end
        end)
        task.wait(0.5) -- HUD ถูกสร้าง/สร้างใหม่ตอนเข้าเกม จึงสแกนซ้ำเรื่อย ๆ
    end
end)

bind(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gpe)
    local t = input.UserInputType
    if t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        if not gpe then mouseShoot = true end
    elseif t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
        if not gpe then mouseAim = true end
    elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2 then padShoot = true
    elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2 then padAim = true
    end
    if snapLock then pcall(snapLock) end -- หันไปล็อคทันทีที่กด
end)

bind(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
    local t = input.UserInputType
    if t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then mouseShoot = false
    elseif t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then mouseAim = false
    elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2 then padShoot = false
    elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2 then padAim = false
    end
    activeTouches[input] = nil -- ปล่อยนิ้ว = เลิกล็อคทันที
end)

-- อาวุธแบบ Tool: Activated = กำลังใช้/ยิง, Deactivated = ปล่อย
local hookedTools = {}
local function hookTool(tool)
    if not tool:IsA("Tool") or hookedTools[tool] then return end
    hookedTools[tool] = true
    tool.Activated:Connect(function()
        activeTool = tool
        if snapLock then pcall(snapLock) end
    end)
    tool.Deactivated:Connect(function() if activeTool == tool then activeTool = nil end end)
    tool.Unequipped:Connect(function() if activeTool == tool then activeTool = nil end end)
end
local function hookCharacter(char)
    if not char then return end
    for _, c in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do hookTool(c) end
    char.ChildAdded:Connect(hookTool)
end
hookCharacter(player.Character)
bind(player.CharacterAdded, function(char) activeTool = nil; hookCharacter(char) end)

local function touchHas(kind)
    for inp, k in pairs(activeTouches) do
        if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
            activeTouches[inp] = nil -- กันค้าง
        elseif k == kind then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

-- true = กำลังเล็ง/ซูม (คลิกขวา / L2 / ปุ่มเล็งบนมือถือ)
local function isAimInputActive()
    if mouseAim then
        local ok, down = pcall(function() return UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) end)
        if ok and not down then mouseAim = false end -- กันค้าง
    end
    if padAim then
        local ok, down = pcall(function() return UserInputService:IsGamepadButtonDown(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2) end)
        if ok and not down then padAim = false end
    end
    return mouseAim or padAim or touchHas("aim")
end

-- true = กำลังยิง/ใช้ (คลิกซ้าย / R2 / ปุ่มยิงบนมือถือ / Tool Activated)
local function isShootInputActive()
    if mouseShoot then
        local ok, down = pcall(function() return UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) end)
        if ok and not down then mouseShoot = false end
    end
    if padShoot then
        local ok, down = pcall(function() return UserInputService:IsGamepadButtonDown(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2) end)
        if ok and not down then padShoot = false end
    end
    if activeTool and activeTool.Parent ~= player.Character then activeTool = nil end
    -- มือถือล้วน: มีแค่ปุ่ม ยิง/ใช้ (Fire / FireLeft) ที่ทำให้ล็อค ปุ่มอื่น/แตะจอ/ลากกล้อง/กระโดดไม่มีผล
    if touchOnlyDevice then return touchHas("shoot") end
    return mouseShoot or padShoot or activeTool ~= nil or touchHas("shoot")
end

-- ล็อคเฉพาะตอน "ยิง/ใช้" เท่านั้น (เล็ง/ซูมอย่างเดียวไม่ล็อค)
local function isLockInputActive()
    return isShootInputActive()
end

local AIM_PARTS = {"UpperTorso", "Torso", "HumanoidRootPart"} -- ล็อคเฉพาะลำตัว (ไม่ล็อคหัว)
local sharedRayParams = RaycastParams.new()
sharedRayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
sharedRayParams.IgnoreWater = true
local sharedRayChar = nil

local function isTargetVisible(char, targetParts)
    local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not Camera or not char then return false, nil end
    local myChar = player.Character
    if sharedRayChar ~= myChar then
        sharedRayChar = myChar
        sharedRayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {myChar}
    end
    local rayParams = sharedRayParams
    for _, partName in ipairs(targetParts) do
        local part = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
            local screenPos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            if onScreen and screenPos.Z > 0 then
                local origin = Camera.CFrame.Position
                local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, part.Position - origin, rayParams)
                if not hit or hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                    return true, part
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false, nil
end

local function getClosestPlayerToCenter()
    local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not Camera then return nil end
    local localChar = player.Character
    local localRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not localRoot then return nil end
    local closestPlayer, closestScore, closestPart = nil, math.huge, nil
    local centerPos = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, Camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
    local aiming = false -- ไม่ใช้โหมดเล็งทั้งจอแล้ว: ล็อคเฉพาะเป้าที่อยู่ตรงหน้า (ในวง FOV) และมองเห็นจริง

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= player then
            if isSameTeam(p) then continue end
            local char = getCharacter(p)
            local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if humanoid and hasHP(humanoid) and root then
                local worldDistance = (root.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if worldDistance <= Settings.MaxDistance then
                    local visible, visiblePart = isTargetVisible(char, AIM_PARTS)
                    if visible and visiblePart then
                        local direction = visiblePart.Position - Camera.CFrame.Position
                        if direction.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                            local dot = math.clamp(Camera.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(direction.Unit), -1, 1)
                            local screenPos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(visiblePart.Position)
                            if onScreen and screenPos.Z > 0 then
                                local screenDistance = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - centerPos).Magnitude
                                local validTarget, score = false, math.huge
                                if aiming then
                                    if dot > 0 then validTarget, score = true, math.deg(math.acos(dot)) end
                                else
                                    if dot > 0 and screenDistance <= Settings.FOVRadius then validTarget, score = true, screenDistance end
                                end
                                if validTarget and score < closestScore then
                                    closestScore, closestPlayer, closestPart = score, p, visiblePart
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closestPlayer, closestPart
end

local lockedTarget = nil
local aimErrWarned = false
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("NapHubAimbot") end)
snapLock = function()
    local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not Camera then return end
    lockedTarget = nil
    if not Settings.AimbotEnabled then return end
    if not isLockInputActive() then return end -- ไม่ยิง/ไม่ใช้ = ไม่ล็อค
    local target, aimTarget = getClosestPlayerToCenter()
    if not target then return end
    -- กันพลาดอีกชั้น: ถ้าเป้าที่ได้ไม่มี HP ให้ยกเลิกล็อค (ปกติถูกข้ามไปแล้วใน getClosestPlayerToCenter)
    local tChar = getCharacter(target)
    if not (tChar and hasHP(tChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))) then return end
    if aimTarget then
        lockedTarget = target
        Camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(Camera.CFrame.Position, aimTarget.Position) -- หันตรงเข้าเป้าทันที
    end
end

RunService:BindToRenderStep("NapHubAimbot", Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value, function()
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        if Settings.ShowFOV and FOVFrame then FOVFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) end
        snapLock()
    end)
    if not ok and not aimErrWarned then
        aimErrWarned = true
        warn("[NapHub Aimbot] error: " .. tostring(err))
    end
end)

-- ==========================================
--  DRAWING ESP : Box / Name / HP / Skeleton (Drawing library)
--  logic + แหล่งข้อมูลทั้งหมดมาจาก warz_esp_lua.txt:
--  หาศัตรูจาก workspace.HeroVisualsLocal > <โฟลเดอร์ผู้เล่น> > LiveAim (โมเดลที่เกมแสดงจริง)
--  ชื่อ = Attribute WarzOwnerUserId, เลือด = RenderHumanoid, โครงกระดูก = Bone Bip01_* (TransformedWorldCFrame)
-- ==========================================
do
    DrawESP.ok = pcall(function()
        local t = Drawing.new("Line")
        t.Visible = false
        t:Remove()
    end)

    local Objects = DrawESP.entries
    local SCAN_INTERVAL = 0.35   -- สแกนหาโมเดลทุก 0.35 วิ (เท่าไฟล์แนบ)
    local DRAW_HEAD = false      -- false = ไม่วาดหัวในโครงกระดูก (เปลี่ยนเป็น true ถ้าอยากให้มีหัวเหมือนไฟล์แนบ)

    local function make(class, properties)
        local object = Drawing.new(class)
        for key, value in pairs(properties) do
            object[key] = value
        end
        return object
    end

    local function makeLine()
        return make("Line", { Visible = false, Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 1, Color = Settings.SkeletonColor })
    end

    local function create(model)
        if Objects[model] then return end
        local obj = {
            Name = make("Text", { Visible = false, Center = true, Outline = true, Size = 14, Color = Settings.BoxColor }),
            Box = make("Square", { Visible = false, Filled = false, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Settings.BoxColor }),
            HPBackground = make("Square", { Visible = false, Filled = true, Color = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20) }),
            HPBar = make("Square", { Visible = false, Filled = true, Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) }),
            HPText = make("Text", { Visible = false, Center = true, Outline = true, Size = 12, Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }),
            Bones = {},
            BoneMap = {}, BoneT = 0,
        }
        for i = 1, 32 do
            obj.Bones[i] = makeLine()
        end
        Objects[model] = obj
    end

    local function hide(obj)
        for key, value in pairs(obj) do
            if key == "Bones" then
                for _, bone in ipairs(value) do bone.Visible = false end
            elseif key ~= "BoneMap" and key ~= "BoneT" then
                value.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end

    local function remove(model)
        local obj = Objects[model]
        if not obj then return end
        for key, value in pairs(obj) do
            if key == "Bones" then
                for _, bone in ipairs(value) do pcall(function() bone:Remove() end) end
            elseif key ~= "BoneMap" and key ~= "BoneT" then
                pcall(function() value:Remove() end)
            end
        end
        Objects[model] = nil
    end

    -- กรอบ 2D จากทุกมุมของพาร์ท (ลูกตรงของโมเดล ยกเว้น RootPart) — ถ้ามีมุมใดอยู่หลังกล้องจะไม่วาด
    local CORNERS = {
        Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1), Vector3.new(-1, -1, 1),
        Vector3.new(-1, 1, -1), Vector3.new(-1, 1, 1),
        Vector3.new(1, -1, -1), Vector3.new(1, -1, 1),
        Vector3.new(1, 1, -1), Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
    }
    local function bounds(model, camera)
        local minX, minY = math.huge, math.huge
        local maxX, maxY = -math.huge, -math.huge
        local hasProjected = false
        for _, part in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
            if not part:IsA("BasePart") then continue end
            if part.Name == "RootPart" then continue end
            local half = part.Size * 0.5
            for _, corner in ipairs(CORNERS) do
                local point = part.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(
                    half.X * corner.X, half.Y * corner.Y, half.Z * corner.Z
                ))
                local projected = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(point)
                if projected.Z <= 0 then return end
                hasProjected = true
                minX = math.min(minX, projected.X)
                minY = math.min(minY, projected.Y)
                maxX = math.max(maxX, projected.X)
                maxY = math.max(maxY, projected.Y)
            end
        end
        if not hasProjected then return end
        local screen = camera.ViewportSize
        if maxX < 0 or minX > screen.X or maxY < 0 or minY > screen.Y then return end
        return Vector2.new(minX, minY), Vector2.new(maxX, maxY)
    end

    local BoneChains = {
        { "Bip01_Pelvis", "Bip01_Spine", "Bip01_Spine1", "Bip01_Neck" },
        { "Bip01_Neck", "Bip01_L_Clavicle", "Bip01_L_UpperArm", "Bip01_L_Forearm", "Bip01_L_Hand" },
        { "Bip01_Neck", "Bip01_R_Clavicle", "Bip01_R_UpperArm", "Bip01_R_Forearm", "Bip01_R_Hand" },
        { "Bip01_Pelvis", "Bip01_L_Thigh", "Bip01_L_Calf", "Bip01_L_Foot" },
        { "Bip01_Pelvis", "Bip01_R_Thigh", "Bip01_R_Calf", "Bip01_R_Foot" },
    }
    if DRAW_HEAD then
        table.insert(BoneChains[1], "Bip01_Head")
    end

    local function boneWorldCF(bone)
        return bone.TransformedWorldCFrame
    end

    local function skeleton(model, obj, camera)
        local color = Settings.SkeletonColor
        local used = 0
        local function drawSegment(a, b)
            if not a or not b or used >= #obj.Bones then return end
            local pa = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(a)
            local pb = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(b)
            if pa.Z <= 0 or pb.Z <= 0 then return end
            used = used + 1
            local line = obj.Bones[used]
            line.From = Vector2.new(pa.X, pa.Y)
            line.To = Vector2.new(pb.X, pb.Y)
            line.Color = color
            line.Visible = true
        end

        -- ถ้าตัวละครมี Bone จริง (แอนิเมชันสด) ใช้ TransformedWorldCFrame ของกระดูกแต่ละท่อน
        -- (เก็บแผนที่ชื่อ->Bone ไว้ 0.5 วิ แทนการไล่ GetDescendants ทุกเฟรม ผลลัพธ์เหมือนเดิม)
        local now = os.clock()
        if (now - obj.BoneT) > 0.5 then
            obj.BoneT = now
            local map = {}
            for _, descendant in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("Bone") then
                    map[descendant.Name] = descendant
                end
            end
            obj.BoneMap = map
        end
        local bones = obj.BoneMap
        local liveBoneCount = 0
        for _, chain in ipairs(BoneChains) do
            for _, name in ipairs(chain) do
                if bones[name] then liveBoneCount = liveBoneCount + 1 end
            end
        end
        if liveBoneCount >= 5 then
            for _, chain in ipairs(BoneChains) do
                local previous
                for _, name in ipairs(chain) do
                    local bone = bones[name]
                    if bone and bone.Parent then
                        local success, cf = pcall(boneWorldCF, bone)
                        if success and cf then
                            if previous then drawSegment(previous, cf.Position) end
                            previous = cf.Position
                        else
                            previous = nil
                        end
                    else
                        previous = nil
                    end
                end
            end
            if used > 0 then return end
        end

        -- ถ้าไม่มี Bone: สร้างโครงกระดูกโดยประมาณจาก CFrame ปัจจุบันของพาร์ท Body/Head/Arms/Legs/RootPart
        local body = model:FindFirstChild("Body")
        local head = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local arms = model:FindFirstChild("Arms")
        local legs = model:FindFirstChild("Legs")
        local root = model:FindFirstChild("RootPart")
        if not body or not body:IsA("BasePart") then return end

        local cf = body.CFrame
        local up, right = cf.UpVector, cf.RightVector
        local torsoHeight = math.max(body.Size.Y, 0.7)
        local torsoWidth = math.max(body.Size.X, 0.7)
        local neck = body.Position + up * (torsoHeight * 0.46)
        local chest = body.Position + up * (torsoHeight * 0.16)
        local waist = body.Position - up * (torsoHeight * 0.18)
        local pelvis = body.Position - up * (torsoHeight * 0.46)
        drawSegment(neck, chest)
        drawSegment(chest, waist)
        drawSegment(waist, pelvis)

        if DRAW_HEAD and head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
            drawSegment(neck, head.Position)
            drawSegment(head.Position, head.Position + head.CFrame.UpVector * (head.Size.Y * 0.38))
        end

        local shoulderWidth = math.max(torsoWidth * 0.52, 0.35)
        local hipWidth = math.max(torsoWidth * 0.30, 0.22)
        local armPart = arms and arms:IsA("BasePart") and arms or body
        local legPart = legs and legs:IsA("BasePart") and legs or root or body
        local armCF, legCF = armPart.CFrame, legPart.CFrame
        local armReach = math.max(armPart.Size.X * 0.48, torsoWidth * 0.75, 0.55)
        local armDrop = math.max(armPart.Size.Y * 0.45, torsoHeight * 0.36, 0.3)
        local legHeight = math.max(legPart.Size.Y, torsoHeight * 0.95, 0.75)
        local legSpread = math.max(legPart.Size.X * 0.25, hipWidth * 0.8)

        for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
            local shoulder = neck + right * shoulderWidth * side
            local elbow = armCF.Position + armCF.RightVector * (armReach * 0.53 * side)
                - armCF.UpVector * (armDrop * 0.28)
            local hand = armCF.Position + armCF.RightVector * (armReach * side)
                - armCF.UpVector * armDrop
            drawSegment(neck, shoulder)
            drawSegment(shoulder, elbow)
            drawSegment(elbow, hand)

            local hip = pelvis + right * hipWidth * side
            local knee = legCF.Position + legCF.RightVector * (legSpread * side)
                - legCF.UpVector * (legHeight * 0.05)
            local foot = legCF.Position + legCF.RightVector * (legSpread * side)
                - legCF.UpVector * (legHeight * 0.48)
            drawSegment(pelvis, hip)
            drawSegment(hip, knee)
            drawSegment(knee, foot)
        end
    end

    -- ชื่อเจ้าของโมเดล: อ่านจาก Attribute WarzOwnerUserId ของโฟลเดอร์ (ดึงชื่อจาก UserId ถ้าไม่อยู่ในเซิร์ฟ)
    local NameCache = {}
    local NamePending = {}

    local function getOwnerName(folder)
        if not folder then return "Unknown" end
        local userId = tonumber(folder:GetAttribute("WarzOwnerUserId"))
        if not userId or userId <= 0 then return folder.Name end

        local owner = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
        if owner then
            NameCache[userId] = owner.Name
            return owner.Name
        end

        if NameCache[userId] ~= nil then return NameCache[userId] or folder.Name end
        if not NamePending[userId] then
            NamePending[userId] = true
            task.spawn(function()
                local success, username = pcall(function()
                    return Players:GetNameFromUserIdAsync(userId)
                end)
                if success and type(username) == "string" and username ~= "" then
                    NameCache[userId] = username
                else
                    NameCache[userId] = false
                end
                NamePending[userId] = nil
            end)
        end
        return folder.Name
    end

    local function update(model, obj, camera)
        hide(obj)
        if not model.Parent or not camera then return end
        local wantBox, wantName, wantHP = Settings.BoxESP, Settings.NameESP, Settings.HPESP
        local wantSkel = Settings.SkeletonESP
        if not (wantBox or wantName or wantHP or wantSkel) then return end

        local root = model:FindFirstChild("RootPart")
        if not root or not root:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        local distance = (root.Position - camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
        if distance > Settings.MaxDistance then return end
        local rootProjected, rootOnScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
        if rootProjected.Z <= 0 or not rootOnScreen then return end

        local topLeft, bottomRight = bounds(model, camera)
        if not topLeft then return end
        local width = bottomRight.X - topLeft.X
        local height = bottomRight.Y - topLeft.Y
        if width <= 1 or height <= 1 then return end
        local centerX = topLeft.X + width * 0.5
        local boxColor = Settings.BoxColor

        if wantName then
            obj.Name.Text = getOwnerName(model.Parent)
            obj.Name.Position = Vector2.new(centerX, topLeft.Y - 17)
            obj.Name.Color = boxColor
            obj.Name.Visible = true
        end
        if wantBox then
            obj.Box.Position = topLeft
            obj.Box.Size = Vector2.new(width, height)
            obj.Box.Color = boxColor
            obj.Box.Visible = true
        end
        if wantHP then
            local humanoid = model:FindFirstChild("RenderHumanoid")
            if humanoid and humanoid:IsA("Humanoid") then
                local maxHealth = math.max(humanoid.MaxHealth, 1)
                local health = math.clamp(humanoid.Health, 0, maxHealth)
                local fraction = health / maxHealth
                local filledHeight = height * fraction
                local barX = topLeft.X - 7
                obj.HPBackground.Position = Vector2.new(barX, topLeft.Y)
                obj.HPBackground.Size = Vector2.new(3, height)
                obj.HPBackground.Visible = true
                obj.HPBar.Position = Vector2.new(barX, bottomRight.Y - filledHeight)
                obj.HPBar.Size = Vector2.new(3, filledHeight)
                obj.HPBar.Color = Color3.fromHSV(fraction / 3, 1, 1)
                obj.HPBar.Visible = filledHeight > 0
                obj.HPText.Text = tostring(math.floor(health + 0.5))
                obj.HPText.Position = Vector2.new(barX - 10, topLeft.Y - 2)
                obj.HPText.Visible = true
            end
        end
        if wantSkel then skeleton(model, obj, camera) end
    end

    local function scan()
        local HeroVisuals = workspace:FindFirstChild("HeroVisualsLocal")
        local active = {}
        if HeroVisuals then
            for _, folder in ipairs(HeroVisuals:GetChildren()) do
                if not folder:IsA("Folder") and not folder:IsA("Model") then continue end
                local ownerId = tonumber(folder:GetAttribute("WarzOwnerUserId"))
                if not Settings.ShowLocalPlayer and LocalPlayer then
                    if ownerId == LocalPlayer.UserId or (not ownerId and folder.Name == LocalPlayer.Name) then
                        continue
                    end
                end
                -- Team Check (ของเดิมในสคริปต์): ซ่อนเพื่อนร่วมทีม
                if ownerId and ownerId > 0 then
                    local ownerPlayer = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(ownerId)
                    if ownerPlayer and ownerPlayer ~= LocalPlayer and isSameTeam(ownerPlayer) then
                        continue
                    end
                end
                local model = folder:FindFirstChild("LiveAim")
                if model and model:IsA("Model") then
                    active[model] = true
                    create(model)
                end
            end
        end
        local stale = {}
        for model in pairs(Objects) do
            if not active[model] then table.insert(stale, model) end
        end
        for _, model in ipairs(stale) do remove(model) end
    end

    function DrawESP.clear()
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("NapHubDrawESP") end)
        local models = {}
        for model in pairs(Objects) do table.insert(models, model) end
        for _, model in ipairs(models) do remove(model) end
    end
    _G.NapHubDrawClear = DrawESP.clear

    if DrawESP.ok then
        local elapsed = 1
        local errWarned = false
        bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function(dt)
            if _G.NapHubRun ~= RUN then
                DrawESP.clear()
                return
            end
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                elapsed = elapsed + dt
                if elapsed >= SCAN_INTERVAL then
                    elapsed = 0
                    scan()
                end
                local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                for model, obj in pairs(Objects) do
                    update(model, obj, camera)
                end
            end)
            if not ok and not errWarned then
                errWarned = true
                warn("[NapHub DrawESP] error: " .. tostring(err))
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- ESP Renderer
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
    local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not Camera then return end
    local currentLockedTarget = lockedTarget

    -- (สำรอง) Box ESP แบบ Frame: ใช้เฉพาะเมื่อ executor ไม่รองรับ Drawing (ปกติใช้ Drawing ด้านบน)
    if not DrawESP.ok then pcall(function()
        for _, tp in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if tp ~= player then
                local box = espBoxes[tp]
                local show = false
                if Settings.BoxESP then
                    local ch = getCharacter(tp)
                    local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hum and root and hasHP(hum) then
                        local top = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                        local bot = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0))
                        if top.Z > 0 and bot.Z > 0 then
                            local h = math.abs(bot.Y - top.Y)
                            local w = h * 0.6
                            box = getBox(tp)
                            box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((top.X + bot.X) / 2 - w / 2, math.min(top.Y, bot.Y))
                            box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
                            box.Visible = true
                            show = true

                            -- อัปเดตหลอด HP
                            local hpBg = box:FindFirstChild("HPBG")
                            local hpFill = hpBg and hpBg:FindFirstChild("HPFill")
                            if hpFill then
                                local pct = math.clamp(hum.Health / math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1), 0, 1)
                                hpFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, pct, 0)
                                hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100), pct)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if not show and box then box.Visible = false end
            end
        end
    end) end

    if not Settings.ESPEnabled then return end

    local myCharacter = getCharacter(player)
    local myRoot = myCharacter and myCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    for targetPlayer, _ in pairs(tracers) do
        if not targetPlayer or not targetPlayer.Parent then
            removeTracer(targetPlayer)
        end
    end

    for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if targetPlayer == player then continue end

        if not tracers[targetPlayer] or not espLabels[targetPlayer] then
            createTracer(targetPlayer)
        end

        local line = tracers[targetPlayer]
        local label = espLabels[targetPlayer]
        if not line or not label then continue end

        if isSameTeam(targetPlayer) then
            line.Visible = false
            label.Visible = false
            continue
        end

        local character = getCharacter(targetPlayer)
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if character and rootPart and humanoid and hasHP(humanoid) then
            local vector, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(rootPart.Position)
            local worldDist = myRoot and (rootPart.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude or 0

            if worldDist > Settings.MaxDistance then
                line.Visible = false
                label.Visible = false
                continue
            end

            if onScreen then
                local startPos = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, Camera.ViewportSize.Y)
                local endPos = Vector2.new(vector.X, vector.Y)

                local distance2D = (endPos - startPos).Magnitude
                local center = (startPos + endPos) / 2
                local angle = math.deg(math.atan2(endPos.Y - startPos.Y, endPos.X - startPos.X))

                local isTargeted = (targetPlayer == currentLockedTarget and Settings.AimbotEnabled)
                local tracerColor = isTargeted and COLORS.cyan or getColorByDistance(worldDist)
                local lineThickness = isTargeted and 3 or 2

                line.Position = UDim2.new(0, center.X, 0, center.Y)
                line.Size = UDim2.new(0, distance2D, 0, lineThickness)
                line.Rotation = angle
                line.BackgroundColor3 = tracerColor
                line.Visible = true

                local statusText = isTargeted and " \u{1F3AF} [LOCKED]" or ""
                label.Position = UDim2.new(0, vector.X, 0, vector.Y - 5)
                label.Text = string.format("%s%s\n%d studs", targetPlayer.Name, statusText, math.floor(worldDist))
                label.TextColor3 = tracerColor
                label.Visible = true
            else
                line.Visible = false
                label.Visible = false
            end
        else
            line.Visible = false
            label.Visible = false
        end
    end
end)

notify("NAP HUB", "โหลดระบบ WarZ Automation & Fishing Hub สำเร็จแล้ว!", 4)

-- ======================================================================================
--  TAB: ตัวละคร (CHARACTER SKIN CHANGER v2 - CLIENT ONLY, ล็อบบี้ + ในเกม)
--  ใช้โมเดลจาก ReplicatedStorage.WarzSkinned สลับให้เฉพาะโมเดลของเราเอง
--  ไม่ส่ง Remote ไปเซิร์ฟเวอร์ -> คนอื่นไม่เห็น
-- ======================================================================================
do
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

    local SkinCfg = { Enabled = false, Selected = "Drift" }

    local FALLBACK_NAMES = {
        "Robot", "DEV", "MilitaryFashion", "FemaleRb01", "Drift", "SnowNinja",
        "RegularGuy", "KaiShadowVan", "MaleRb01", "Demon", "Vampire", "BlackKnight",
        "HeistMask", "StreetGoth", "DarkViking", "SpidyVerse",
    }
    local BASE_PARTS = { "Head", "Body", "Arms", "Legs" }
    local TAG = "NAPHUB_SkinClone"

    local applied = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local lastFallback = 0

    local function getTemplateNames()
        local out = {}
        local skinned = RS:FindFirstChild("WarzSkinned")
        if skinned then
            for _, m in ipairs(skinned:GetChildren()) do
                if m:IsA("Model") and m:FindFirstChild("RootPart") and m:FindFirstChild("Body") then
                    table.insert(out, m.Name)
                end
            end
        end
        if #out == 0 then out = table.clone(FALLBACK_NAMES) end
        table.sort(out)
        return out
    end

    local function isRigModel(m)
        if not (m and m:IsA("Model")) then return false end
        local body = m:FindFirstChild("Body")
        local root = m:FindFirstChild("RootPart")
        return root ~= nil and root:IsA("BasePart") and body ~= nil and body:IsA("BasePart")
    end

    -- เจ้าของโมเดล: ไล่ขึ้นไปหา Attribute WarzOwnerUserId ที่ตัวมันเองหรือพ่อแม่
    local function ownerOf(m)
        local cur = m
        while cur and cur ~= workspace do
            local oid = tonumber(cur:GetAttribute("WarzOwnerUserId"))
            if oid and oid > 0 then return oid end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function nameMatchesMe(m)
        local cur = m
        while cur and cur ~= workspace do
            if cur.Name == LocalPlayer.Name then return true end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function collectFrom(root, mineOnly, out, seen)
        if not root then return end
        local function consider(d)
            if seen[d] or not isRigModel(d) then return end
            local oid = ownerOf(d)
            if oid then
                if oid ~= LocalPlayer.UserId then return end
            elseif mineOnly and not nameMatchesMe(d) then
                return
            end
            seen[d] = true
            table.insert(out, d)
        end
        consider(root)
        for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("Model") then consider(d) end
        end
    end

    local function findMyRigs()
        local rigs, seen = {}, {}
        -- ในเกม
        local hv = workspace:FindFirstChild("HeroVisualsLocal")
        if hv then collectFrom(hv, true, rigs, seen) end
        -- ล็อบบี้ (ฉากหน้าเมนู)
        local fe = workspace:FindFirstChild("FrontEndStage")
        if fe then
            for _, d in ipairs(fe:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("Model") and isRigModel(d) and not seen[d] then
                    local oid = ownerOf(d)
                    if not oid or oid == LocalPlayer.UserId then
                        seen[d] = true
                        table.insert(rigs, d)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        -- Character ของเราเอง (บางเวอร์ชันเกมวางโมเดลไว้ในนี้)
        if LocalPlayer.Character then collectFrom(LocalPlayer.Character, false, rigs, seen) end
        -- สำรอง: สแกนทั้ง workspace หาโมเดลที่อยู่ติดตัวละครเรา / ชื่อเรา
        local inGame = 0
        for _, r in ipairs(rigs) do
            if not r:FindFirstAncestor("FrontEndStage") then inGame = inGame + 1 end
        end
        if inGame == 0 and os.clock() - lastFallback > 1.5 then
            lastFallback = os.clock()
            local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("Model") and isRigModel(d) and not seen[d] then
                    local oid = ownerOf(d)
                    local ok = false
                    if oid then
                        ok = (oid == LocalPlayer.UserId)
                    elseif nameMatchesMe(d) then
                        ok = true
                    elseif hrp then
                        ok = (d.RootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 14
                    end
                    if ok then seen[d] = true; table.insert(rigs, d) end
                end
            end
        end
        return rigs
    end

    -- ==================================================================
    --  ค้นหาเท็กซ์เจอร์ของตัวละครแต่ละตัวจากข้อมูลที่เกมใช้เอง (require ModuleScript)
    --  ReplicatedStorage.Shared.WarzTextureIds / Shared.warz.WarzHeroes(_V2)
    -- ==================================================================
    local function norm(x) return (tostring(x):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")) end

    local function assetUrl(v)
        if type(v) == "number" and v > 100000 then return "rbxassetid://" .. string.format("%d", v) end
        if type(v) == "string" then
            if v:find("rbxassetid://", 1, true) or v:find("rbxasset://", 1, true) or v:find("http", 1, true) then return v end
            if v:match("^%d+$") and #v >= 6 then return "rbxassetid://" .. v end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local HERO_ID_FIELDS = { "Id", "ID", "Name", "Key", "Model", "Hero", "HeroId", "Skin", "ModelName", "DisplayName", "Title", "Survivor" }
    local leafCache = nil

    local function buildLeafCache()
        local names, nameSet = getTemplateNames(), {}
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do nameSet[norm(n)] = n end
        local leaves = {}
        local shared = RS:FindFirstChild("Shared")
        local tidMod = shared and shared:FindFirstChild("WarzTextureIds")
        local tidMap = nil
        if tidMod then
            local okT, resT = pcall(require, tidMod)
            if okT and type(resT) == "table" then tidMap = resT end
        end
        local function walk(t, path, hero, depth, visited)
            if depth > 7 or visited[t] then return end
            visited[t] = true
            for _, f in ipairs(HERO_ID_FIELDS) do
                local fv = rawget(t, f)
                if type(fv) == "string" and nameSet[norm(fv)] then hero = nameSet[norm(fv)] break end
            end
            for k, v in pairs(t) do
                local ks = tostring(k)
                local h = hero
                if nameSet[norm(ks)] then h = nameSet[norm(ks)] end
                if type(v) == "table" then
                    walk(v, path .. "." .. ks, h, depth + 1, visited)
                else
                    local url = assetUrl(v)
                    if not url and type(v) == "string" and tidMap and tidMap[v] then url = assetUrl(tidMap[v]) end
                    if url then table.insert(leaves, { hero = h, path = path .. "." .. ks, url = url }) end
                end
            end
        end
        local sources = {
            { RS:FindFirstChild("Shared") and RS.Shared:FindFirstChild("WarzTextureIds"), "TextureIds" },
            { RS:FindFirstChild("Shared") and RS.Shared:FindFirstChild("warz") and RS.Shared.warz:FindFirstChild("WarzHeroes"), "Heroes" },
            { RS:FindFirstChild("Shared") and RS.Shared:FindFirstChild("warz") and RS.Shared.warz:FindFirstChild("WarzHeroes_V2"), "HeroesV2" },
        }
        for _, src in ipairs(sources) do
            if src[1] then
                local ok, mod = pcall(require, src[1])
                if ok and type(mod) == "table" then pcall(walk, mod, src[2], nil, 0, {}) end
            end
        end
        -- ชื่อคีย์ใน WarzTextureIds ที่มีชื่อตัวละคร เช่น femalerb01_body_d
        if tidMap then
            for k, v in pairs(tidMap) do
                local url = assetUrl(v)
                if url then
                    local kn = norm(k)
                    for _, nm in ipairs(names) do
                        local nn = norm(nm)
                        if #nn >= 5 and kn:find(nn, 1, true) then
                            table.insert(leaves, { hero = nm, path = "TextureIds." .. tostring(k), url = url })
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        -- เรียกฟังก์ชัน WarzHeroes.Get(id) ของเกมโดยตรง
        local warzF = shared and shared:FindFirstChild("warz")
        for _, mname in ipairs({ "WarzHeroes", "WarzHeroes_V2" }) do
            local hm = warzF and warzF:FindFirstChild(mname)
            if hm then
                local okH, H = pcall(require, hm)
                if okH and type(H) == "table" and type(H.Get) == "function" then
                    for _, nm in ipairs(names) do
                        local id = nm
                        if type(H.ResolveId) == "function" then
                            local okR, rid = pcall(H.ResolveId, nm)
                            if okR and rid then id = rid end
                        end
                        local okG, def = pcall(H.Get, id)
                        if okG and type(def) == "table" then
                            pcall(walk, def, mname .. "." .. nm, nm, 0, {})
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return leaves
    end

    local function pickLeaf(hero, part, kinds, excl)
        leafCache = leafCache or buildLeafCache()
        local pn = norm(part)
        local best, bestScore
        for _, lf in ipairs(leafCache) do
            local pp = norm(lf.path)
            local skip = false
            if excl then for _, kw in ipairs(excl) do if pp:find(kw, 1, true) then skip = true end end end
            if lf.hero == hero and not skip then
                local score = 0
                if pp:find(pn, 1, true) then score = score + 10 end
                local hit = false
                for _, kw in ipairs(kinds) do if pp:find(kw, 1, true) then hit = true score = score + 5 end end
                if hit or (#kinds == 0) then
                    if score > 0 and (not bestScore or score > bestScore) then best, bestScore = lf, score end
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    -- ==================================================================
    --  ดึงเท็กซ์เจอร์ "ของจริง" จากโมเดลที่เกมวาดใน ViewportFrame ของ UI
    --  (ร้านค้า Robux / หน้าเลือกตัวละคร HeroGrid) แล้วเก็บไว้ใช้
    -- ==================================================================
    local texCache = {}   -- texCache[hero][part] = { sa = SurfaceAppearance|nil, tid = string|nil }
    local modelCache = {} -- modelCache[hero] = { model = Model(clone), score = n }  โมเดลสมบูรณ์จาก UI (มีผม/หน้า/เท็กซ์เจอร์)
    local GEAR_PREFIX = { "ARMOR", "MOTO_", "HAND_", "HEADHELMET" }
    local function isGearName(n)
        for _, pre in ipairs(GEAR_PREFIX) do
            if n:sub(1, #pre) == pre then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function modelScore(m)
        local sc = 0
        for _, c in ipairs(m:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("MeshPart") and not isGearName(c.Name) then
                if c:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance") or c.TextureID ~= "" then sc = sc + 1 end
                if c:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal") then sc = sc + 1 end
            end
        end
        return sc
    end

    local function heroNameOf(model, nameSet)
        if nameSet[norm(model.Name)] then return nameSet[norm(model.Name)] end
        local cur = model.Parent
        while cur and cur ~= game do
            local n = cur.Name
            local h = n:match("^Hero_(.+)$") or n:match("^Select_(.+)$")
            if h and nameSet[norm(h)] then return nameSet[norm(h)] end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function scanUI()
        local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return 0 end
        local names, nameSet = getTemplateNames(), {}
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do nameSet[norm(n)] = n end
        local added = 0
        for _, vp in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
            if vp:IsA("ViewportFrame") then
                for _, m in ipairs(vp:GetDescendants()) do
                    if m:IsA("Model") and m:FindFirstChild("Body") then
                        local hero = heroNameOf(m, nameSet)
                        if hero then
                            local sc = modelScore(m)
                            if sc > 0 and (not modelCache[hero] or sc > modelCache[hero].score) then
                                local okC, cl = pcall(function() return m:Clone() end)
                                if okC and cl then
                                    modelCache[hero] = { model = cl, score = sc }
                                    added = added + 1
                                end
                            end
                            texCache[hero] = texCache[hero] or {}
                            for _, pn in ipairs(BASE_PARTS) do
                                local part = m:FindFirstChild(pn)
                                if part and part:IsA("MeshPart") and not texCache[hero][pn] then
                                    local sa = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance")
                                    if sa then
                                        texCache[hero][pn] = { sa = sa:Clone() }
                                        added = added + 1
                                    elseif part.TextureID ~= "" then
                                        texCache[hero][pn] = { tid = part.TextureID }
                                        added = added + 1
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return added
    end

    local function cachedHeroCount()
        local n = 0
        for _, parts in pairs(texCache) do if parts.Body then n = n + 1 end end
        return n
    end

    -- สแกนเรื่อย ๆ จนกว่าจะครบทุกตัว (เปิดหน้าร้านค้า/หน้าเลือกตัวละครแล้วจะเก็บให้เอง)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(1.5)
            if cachedHeroCount() < #getTemplateNames() then pcall(scanUI) end
        end
    end)

    local function heroDefTextures(hero)
        local shared = RS:FindFirstChild("Shared")
        local warzF = shared and shared:FindFirstChild("warz")
        if not warzF then return nil end
        for _, mname in ipairs({ "WarzHeroes", "WarzHeroes_V2" }) do
            local hm = warzF:FindFirstChild(mname)
            if hm then
                local okH, H = pcall(require, hm)
                if okH and type(H) == "table" and type(H.Get) == "function" then
                    local id = hero
                    if type(H.ResolveId) == "function" then
                        local okR, rid = pcall(H.ResolveId, hero)
                        if okR and rid then id = rid end
                    end
                    local okG, def = pcall(H.Get, id)
                    if okG and type(def) == "table" and type(def.textures) == "table" and next(def.textures) then
                        return def.textures
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function resolveSA(hero, part, old)
        -- -1) เท็กซ์เจอร์ตามที่เกมกำหนดไว้ในข้อมูลฮีโร่ (แม่นที่สุด)
        local tx = heroDefTextures(hero)
        if tx then
            local d = tx[part .. "D"] or tx.BodyD
            local n = tx[part .. "N"] or tx.BodyN
            if type(d) == "string" and d ~= "" then
                local sa = Instance.new("SurfaceAppearance")
                sa.Name = "SA"
                sa.ColorMap = d
                if type(n) == "string" and n ~= "" and n ~= d then sa.NormalMap = n end
                return sa
            end
        end
        -- 0) เท็กซ์เจอร์จริงที่เก็บจาก UI ของเกม
        if not (texCache[hero] and texCache[hero][part]) then pcall(scanUI) end
        local hit = texCache[hero] and texCache[hero][part]
        if hit then
            if hit.sa then return hit.sa:Clone() end
            if hit.tid then
                local sa = Instance.new("SurfaceAppearance")
                sa.Name = "SA"
                sa.ColorMap = hit.tid
                return sa
            end
        end
        -- 1) WarzLook มี SA ตั้งชื่อตามตัวละคร เช่น DriftHeadSA
        local look = RS:FindFirstChild("WarzLook")
        if look then
            local want = norm(hero .. part .. "SA")
            for _, c in ipairs(look:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") and norm(c.Name) == want then
                    return c:Clone()
                end
            end
        end
        -- 2) ข้อมูลฮีโร่/เท็กซ์เจอร์ที่เกมใช้
        local color = pickLeaf(hero, part, { "color", "tex", "albedo", "diffuse", "atlas", "map" }, { "normal", "metal", "rough" })
        if not color then color = pickLeaf(hero, part, {}, { "normal", "metal", "rough" }) end
        if color then
            local sa = Instance.new("SurfaceAppearance")
            sa.Name = "SA"
            sa.ColorMap = color.url
            local nrm = pickLeaf(hero, part, { "normal" })
            if nrm and nrm.url ~= color.url then sa.NormalMap = nrm.url end
            local met = pickLeaf(hero, part, { "metal" })
            if met and met.url ~= color.url then sa.MetalnessMap = met.url end
            local rou = pickLeaf(hero, part, { "rough" })
            if rou and rou.url ~= color.url then sa.RoughnessMap = rou.url end
            warn("[NAPHUB-Skin] " .. hero .. "." .. part .. " texture <- " .. color.path)
            return sa
        end
        -- 3) สำรอง
        warn("[NAPHUB-Skin] ไม่เจอเท็กซ์เจอร์ " .. hero .. "." .. part .. " (ใช้ผิวพื้นฐานแทน) เปิดหน้าร้านค้า Robux > ตัวละคร หรือหน้าเลือกตัวละครสักครั้งให้เกมโหลดโมเดล แล้วกดรีเฟรช")
        local base = look and look:FindFirstChild("BodySA")
        if base then return base:Clone() end
        local sa = old and old:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance")
        return sa and sa:Clone() or nil
    end

    local function boneMap(model)
        local map = {}
        local root = model:FindFirstChild("RootPart")
        if root then
            for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("Bone") then map[d.Name] = d end
            end
        end
        return map
    end

    local function restore(rig)
        local st = applied[rig]
        if not st then return end
        applied[rig] = nil
        for _, c in ipairs(st.clones) do
            if c and c.Parent then c:Destroy() end
        end
        for part, tr in pairs(st.hidden) do
            if part and part.Parent then part.Transparency = tr; part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end
        end
        for b, cf in pairs(st.boneOrig) do
            if b and b.Parent then pcall(function() b.CFrame = cf end) end
        end
        for v, val in pairs(st.poseOrig) do
            if v and v.Parent then pcall(function() v.Value = val end) end
        end
    end

    -- ชิ้นส่วนใหม่ควรโชว์ไหม: ล็อบบี้โชว์ทั้งหมด / ในแมตช์ FPS โชว์แค่แขน / TPS โชว์ทั้งตัว
    local function cloneWanted(st, key)
        if st.always or key == "Arms" then return 0 end
        local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
        local tps = ch and ch:GetAttribute("WarzTps")
        return (tps == false) and 1 or 0
    end

    local warnedNoTex = {}
    local function apply(rig, name)
        local skinned = RS:FindFirstChild("WarzSkinned")
        local tpl = skinned and skinned:FindFirstChild(name)
        local root = rig:FindFirstChild("RootPart")
        if not (tpl and root) then return false end

        local st = applied[rig]
        if st and st.name == name then
            local alive = (#st.clones > 0)
            for _, c in ipairs(st.clones) do
                if not c.Parent then alive = false break end
            end
            if alive then
                for part in pairs(st.hidden) do
                    if part.Parent then part.Transparency = 1; part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
                end
                return true
            end
        end
        restore(rig)

        st = { name = name, clones = {}, hidden = {}, boneOrig = {}, boneTarget = {}, poseOrig = {}, mirror = {}, always = (rig:FindFirstAncestor("FrontEndStage") ~= nil) }

        -- 1) ปรับโครงกระดูกของเราให้ตรงกับของตัวละครที่เลือก (สัดส่วนหัว/ตัว/ขา ไม่เพี้ยน)
        local liveBones, tplBones = boneMap(rig), boneMap(tpl)
        for n, lb in pairs(liveBones) do
            local tb = tplBones[n]
            if tb then
                st.boneOrig[lb] = lb.CFrame
                st.boneTarget[lb] = tb.CFrame
            end
        end
        local lp, tp = rig:FindFirstChild("InitialPoses"), tpl:FindFirstChild("InitialPoses")
        if lp and tp then
            for _, v in ipairs(lp:GetChildren()) do
                local tv = tp:FindFirstChild(v.Name)
                if tv and v:IsA("CFrameValue") and tv:IsA("CFrameValue") then
                    st.poseOrig[v] = v.Value
                    pcall(function() v.Value = tv.Value end)
                end
            end
        end
        for lb, cf in pairs(st.boneTarget) do pcall(function() lb.CFrame = cf end) end

        -- 2) ใส่ชิ้นส่วนร่างกายของตัวละครที่เลือก (หัว/ตัว/แขน/ขา + ผม ฯลฯ)
        if not modelCache[name] then pcall(scanUI) end
        local cached = modelCache[name]
        if not cached and not warnedNoTex[name] then
            warnedNoTex[name] = true
            pcall(notify, "ตัวละคร", name .. ": ยังไม่มีเท็กซ์เจอร์ — เปิดหน้า 'เลือกตัวละคร/ร้านค้า' ให้เห็นตัวนี้ 1 ครั้ง แล้วกดรีเฟรช", 6)
        end
        local sources = {}   -- { part = MeshPart, fromUI = bool }
        if cached then
            for _, c in ipairs(cached.model:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("MeshPart") and not isGearName(c.Name) then
                    table.insert(sources, { part = c, fromUI = true })
                end
            end
        else
            for _, pn in ipairs(BASE_PARTS) do
                local src = tpl:FindFirstChild(pn)
                if src and src:IsA("MeshPart") then table.insert(sources, { part = src, fromUI = false }) end
            end
            if name ~= "DEV" then
                for _, c in ipairs(tpl:GetChildren()) do
                    if c:IsA("MeshPart") and c:GetAttribute("WarzHeroPart") and not table.find(BASE_PARTS, c.Name) then
                        table.insert(sources, { part = c, fromUI = false })
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- ซ่อนชิ้นส่วนร่างกาย/ผมเดิมของตัวละครที่เกมใส่อยู่
        for _, ch in ipairs(rig:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("BasePart") and not ch:GetAttribute(TAG)
                and (table.find(BASE_PARTS, ch.Name) or ch:GetAttribute("WarzHeroPart")) then
                st.hidden[ch] = ch.Transparency
                ch.Transparency = 1
                ch.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            end
        end

        for _, srcInfo in ipairs(sources) do
            local src = srcInfo.part
            local old = rig:FindFirstChild(src.Name)
            local c = src:Clone()
            c.Name = "NAP_" .. src.Name
            c:SetAttribute(TAG, true)

            local texPart = table.find(BASE_PARTS, src.Name) and src.Name or src:GetAttribute("WarzHeroPart")
            if not srcInfo.fromUI and texPart
                and not c:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance") and c.TextureID == "" then
                local sa = resolveSA(name, texPart, old)
                if sa then sa.Parent = c end
            end

            if old and old:IsA("BasePart") and not old:GetAttribute(TAG) then c.CFrame = old.CFrame end
            if not srcInfo.fromUI then c.Transparency = 0 end
            c.Anchored = false
            c.CanCollide = false
            c.CanTouch = false
            c.CanQuery = false
            c.Massless = true
            for _, m in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                if m:IsA("Motor6D") then
                    m.Part0 = root
                    m.Part1 = c
                end
            end
            local mk = table.find(BASE_PARTS, src.Name) and src.Name or src:GetAttribute("WarzHeroPart")
            if mk then
                st.mirror[c] = mk
                c.Transparency = cloneWanted(st, mk)
            end
            c.Parent = rig
            table.insert(st.clones, c)
        end
        applied[rig] = st
        return #st.clones > 0
    end

    local function restoreAll()
        for rig in pairs(applied) do restore(rig) end
    end

    -- กันเกมรีเซ็ตท่าโครงกระดูกกลับ
    game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        for _, st in pairs(applied) do
            for lb, cf in pairs(st.boneTarget) do
                if lb.Parent and lb.CFrame ~= cf then pcall(function() lb.CFrame = cf end) end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- บังคับทุกเฟรมหลังสคริปต์เกม: ซ่อนตัวเดิม + ตั้งความโปร่งใสของตัวใหม่
    pcall(function() game:GetService("RunService"):UnbindFromRenderStep("NapHubSkinEnforce") end)
    game:GetService("RunService"):BindToRenderStep("NapHubSkinEnforce", Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value, function()
        for _, st in pairs(applied) do
            for part in pairs(st.hidden) do
                if part.Parent then
                    if part.Transparency ~= 1 then part.Transparency = 1 end
                    if part.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= 1 then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
                end
            end
            for c, key in pairs(st.mirror) do
                if c.Parent then
                    local w = cloneWanted(st, key)
                    if c.Transparency ~= w then c.Transparency = w end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.25)
            pcall(function()
                if not SkinCfg.Enabled then
                    if next(applied) ~= nil then restoreAll() end
                    return
                end
                local rigs = findMyRigs()
                local seen = {}
                for _, rig in ipairs(rigs) do
                    seen[rig] = true
                    apply(rig, SkinCfg.Selected)
                end
                for rig in pairs(applied) do
                    if not seen[rig] and not rig.Parent then restore(rig) end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    local SkinTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "ตัวละคร", Icon = "user" })

    SkinTab:Section({ Title = "🧍 เปลี่ยนตัวละคร (เห็นแค่เรา · ล็อบบี้ + ในเกม)" })

    SkinTab:Toggle({
        Title = "เปิดใช้สกินตัวละคร  ·  คนอื่นไม่เห็น",
        Value = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            SkinCfg.Enabled = state == true
            if not SkinCfg.Enabled then pcall(restoreAll) end
        end,
    })

    SkinTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "เลือกตัวละคร (ทุกตัวในเกม ไม่ต้องซื้อ)",
        Values = getTemplateNames(),
        Value = SkinCfg.Selected,
        Callback = function(v)
            if type(v) == "table" then v = v[1] end
            if v then
                SkinCfg.Selected = tostring(v)
                warnedNoTex[SkinCfg.Selected] = nil
                pcall(scanUI)
            end
        end,
    })

    -- (ซ่อนปุ่มสแกน/รีเฟรช/Debug ออกจากเมนูแล้ว — ระบบสแกนเท็กซ์เจอร์ยังทำงานอัตโนมัติ)

end
